Ref2VA was not distilled"* — and the i2v template actually **wires `first_frame`**, so it is the one to believe (owner called this correctly). Both templates ship in `comfyui_workflow_templates_json` inside `python_embeded`, which is where to look before trusting a candidate's prose.
- **[video_fastvideo_fasth3_t2v](workflows/video_fastvideo_fasth3_t2v/)** — text to video with sound, 8 steps, no uploads.
- **[video_fastvideo_fasth3_i2v](workflows/video_fastvideo_fasth3_i2v/)** — the same fast checkpoint starting from a picture, converted from ComfyUI's own i2v template. ⚠ **This paragraph is superseded — see the 2026-09-22 entry above.** It originally read "the **first frame is required**, not optional: the template derives the video's width and height from it (`LoadImage → ImageScaleToTotalPixels → GetImageSize`), so unlinking it would break the graph". That was true only of the template's sizing chain, not of the model: the node declares both keyframes optional and resizes them itself, so the chain was swapped for a `ResolutionSelector` and **both frames are now optional**. ★ Prompt form matters for this model: bind the picture to the timeline (*"For the target video, at 0.00 seconds into the target video, <Picture 1> (from [Shot 1]) is fully referenced."*) and then write one `integrated_multimodal_description:` block covering shot, action and **sound**.
- *(An earlier `video_minimax_h3_flf2v_turbo` was built and then removed: it existed only because of the stale t2v note, and the official i2v template does the same job on the fast checkpoint.)*
- ★ **Calibration now measures VRAM** ([benchmarkService.js](server/benchmark/benchmarkService.js) `_watchVram`) → `vramPeakGb` / `vramResidentGb` / `vramCardPeakGb` in runtime.json, preferred over the static estimate on the admin card and by the lane admission check. **This is what finally gave TRELLIS2 / Pixal3D a VRAM figure**: their graphs name a HuggingFace *repo* (`microsoft/TRELLIS.2-4B`, `TencentARC/Pixal3D`), not files, so nothing was there to sum — and the repos are 17 GB and 23 GB on disk while the pipelines stage through them. **Measured: `3d_pixal3d_trellis2_image_to_model` 9.12 GB, `3d_pixal3d_image_to_mesh` 16.94 GB.** ★ `torch_vram` alone is NOT enough: these nodes allocate outside PyTorch's allocator (torch reported 3.0 GB for a run in which the card grew 9.1 GB), so the sampler takes a baseline before the run and reports the larger of "torch held" and "the card grew". Under-reporting is the dangerous direction — it would let a lane in that does not fit. (Per-process attribution is impossible here: `nvidia-smi --query-compute-apps` returns `[N/A]` on Windows/WDDM.) Calibration also now runs on the workflow's **own lane** when it has one.
- **Rig note:** `3d_trellis2_image_to_textured_mesh` still cannot be auto-calibrated — the run returns in ~1 s with 0 steps even though both its literal seeds are randomized, so it has timings from an earlier good run and no VRAM figure. Calibrate it from a real booked job if that number is wanted.

## Latest progress (2026-09-21)

**Parallel lanes — one machine serves several workflows at once, gated by a VRAM estimate (owner request, 2026-09-21)** — owner: *"see the admin panel and select a model to run in one instance… then connect to the admin and serve another model in another instance with parallel inference if it fits the vram"*, and *"we do not want to touch the comfy portable installation"*. `npm run dev` is unchanged; everything is driven from the admin panel. Branch `feature/parallel-lanes`.
- ★ **A lane = a workflow + its own ComfyUI + its own executor** ([laneManager.js](server/lanes/laneManager.js)). The first lane keeps the configured port and the install's own directories, so a single-lane machine behaves exactly as before; every extra lane gets the next port and **its own `--user-directory` / `--temp-directory` under `.comfyq-lanes/` at the repo root**. Both are mandatory, not tidiness: ComfyUI 0.37 takes an exclusive `FileLock` on `<user-dir>/comfyui.db` with `timeout=0` (a second process dies instantly) and **`rmtree`s its temp dir at startup** (`main.py:499`). The portable install is never written to — only extra command-line flags — and `models/` + all 47 custom nodes stay shared because `--base-directory` is deliberately NOT used. `--vram-headroom` asks each extra ComfyUI to leave room for its neighbours (its DynamicVRAM reads real free memory via NVML, so lanes negotiate rather than OOM).
- ★ **One lane per workflow** is the invariant that makes it safe: `queue.findReady(now, workflowIds)` now takes a filter and each lane's `JobExecutor` claims only its own workflow's jobs, so two lanes can never see the same row and no lease is needed. Booking a workflow that no lane serves is **refused at `book_job`** — it would otherwise sit SCHEDULED for ever, since no executor would claim it.
- ★ **VRAM estimate, per workflow, per machine** ([vramEstimate.js](server/workflows/vramEstimate.js), 16 tests). Sums the model files on the graph's **active path**, resolving `ComfySwitchNode` and following only the taken branch — its inputs are `lazy=True` and ComfyUI loads just the selected side (`comfy_extras/nodes_logic.py:86-116`). Measured across the library, branch-awareness changes 14 of 58 bundles by up to **9.6 GB** (LTX 2.5 union-control 47.3 → 37.7). An unreadable switch counts **both** sides rather than guessing low. Three bundles (pixal3d, trellis2, liveportrait) name no model file at all and report **"VRAM unknown", never 0 GB**; unresolved filenames are listed, not silently dropped. The yardstick is `config.instance.vramGb`, detected per machine, so the same bundle reads differently on the 5090 (32 GB), the A6000 (48) and the A6000 Pro (96). **Measured here:** SeedVR2-3B 3.69 · SAM3 1.63 · Flux2 Klein 17.29 (t2i and edit are byte-identical files) · Krea 2 17.8 · Ideogram 4 27.46 (two UNETs) · Flux2 Dev **66.39**, which exceeds this card and streams from RAM.
- **Admin panel:** every workflow card carries a VRAM chip — **green when it fits beside what is running, red when it does not** — and the per-card button becomes **"Serve alongside"**, clickable only when it fits, greyed with the numbers in its tooltip when it doesn't (`needs 27.46 GB, only 9.02 GB free of 32`). A summary line reads *"Serving N workflows in parallel · X of Y GB of models · Z GB free for another"*. **Adding a lane does NOT restart the server**, unlike "Activate & serve" — open student tabs are not disturbed. Verified live with the 4K upscaler served: 12 workflows green and clickable, 45 red and greyed, 3 "unknown".
- **Student booking** gained a **model picker** when more than one lane runs: the broadcast carries a `lanes[]` array (each with its own `parameter_map`, description and guides) while `workflow_info` still points at the first lane, so a client that knows nothing about lanes keeps working. Picking a model rebuilds the form for it — verified live: SeedVR2's *Source image / Target resolution / Seed* became SAM3's *Source video / What to segment / Detection threshold* — and the booking sends `workflow_id` so it lands in that lane's queue.
- **Lanes survive a restart.** Extra lanes are remembered in `workflows.extraLaneWorkflowIds` and reopened at boot, because nodemon reloads and crashes would otherwise drop a class to one model silently. On restore a lane **adopts** a ComfyUI still listening on its port when the argv proves it is its own (the lane's unique user dir), saving a 90 s cold start; anything else on that port is refused rather than shared, since a cancel or a `/free` in one lane would hit the other.
- ★ **Rig lesson (cost an hour):** the first lane died seconds after starting because its state lived in `server/data/`, and the new ComfyUI's Manager immediately wrote `__manager/cache/*.json` there — **nodemon watches `server/` and restarts on `.json`**, so ComfyQ restarted and killed the child it had just spawned. Lane state moved to `.comfyq-lanes/` (outside the watch tree and outside the install) and `--ignore server/data` was added to the nodemon script. **Nothing under `server/data/` should ever trigger a reload.**
- **Honest expectation:** two jobs on one GPU do not each run at full speed — they time-share the SMs. The wins are two models resident (no 22–160 s reload when alternating), two independent queues, and overlap of the non-GPU phases. Pair a heavy model with a light one — but ⚠ **the example this entry originally gave is wrong**: "Flux2 Klein + SeedVR2-3B = 21 GB" was a static estimate; **measured it is 30.17 GB and does not fit on the 5090** (see the 2026-09-22 backfill entry above — the estimator under-reported SeedVR2-3B by 3.4×). Klein + the PiD upscaler (29.85) or Krea 2 + SeedVR2-3B (29.56) do fit. Ideogram 4 or Flux2 Dev own the card alone on 32 GB.

## Latest progress (2026-09-18)

**Fleet-scale client fixes — media caching, delta broadcasts, background/idle tabs, restart-proof serving (owner report, 2026-09-18)** — owner: *"several tabs open and the app begins to lag, loading the medias can be too long… the app has some limits when we have a lot of machines and a lot of generation"*, plus "autoclose a tab when no job is scheduled and idle > 5 min" and the workflow-switch refresh again. Four causes, all measured on the rig:
- ★ **Every progress tick pushed the WHOLE job list to every tab.** `_emitState` sent a full snapshot — **369 KB with only 76 jobs in the DB** — and a running job ticks ~4×/s, so 10 open tabs meant ~14 MB/s of JSON to encode, ship and re-parse. Now the bus keeps what it last sent (`_sentJobs` per job id + `_sentHead` per top-level field) and emits **`state_patch`** with just the jobs that changed, the ids that went, and the head fields that moved, stamped with a `seq`; the full `state_update` is only sent on connect, on `request_state` (throttled 2 s/socket), when >60 jobs changed at once, and as a 60 s self-heal ([realtimeBus.js](server/realtime/realtimeBus.js)). Client side [mergeState.js](client/src/utils/mergeState.js) gained `applyPatch`, which keeps the identity of every untouched job (so only the affected card re-renders) and returns `prev` when a patch changes nothing; a `seq` gap (only possible across a reconnect) asks for a fresh snapshot instead of drifting. **Measured live: a head-only change went 369 070 B → 145 B (2500×).** Tests: `client/src/utils/mergeState.test.mjs` (7 checks, added to `test:storyboard`) — it caught that `seq` must be stripped at the merge boundary, or every periodic snapshot would re-render the whole page for nothing.
- ★ **Results were served with `Cache-Control: public, max-age=0`** (Express `sendFile`'s default), so every tile revalidated on every render — one round trip per result per tab, which is why "loading the medias" dragged. Output filenames are namespaced with user+date+job id, i.e. immutable, so [mediaStore.js](server/media/mediaStore.js) now sends **`public, max-age=31536000, immutable`** for anything under the output dir (`temp/` gets 60 s, it isn't ours to promise) and `/input-media` does the same for uploads ([uploads.js](server/routes/uploads.js)) — both with `sendFile(..., { cacheControl: false })`, else sendFile overwrites the header. **Measured in Chrome: on a reload 20 of 24 media responses came from cache with 0 revalidations** (before: 24 conditional GETs). This is the client-side persistence the owner asked for — note a **service worker is not an option** (plain HTTP off localhost is not a secure context), the HTTP cache is.
- ★ **Background tabs kept rendering.** `state` updates now go through `applyState`, which folds into a ref and **skips the re-render while `document.hidden`**, painting once on return ([SocketContext.jsx](client/src/context/SocketContext.jsx)). **Measured: a job appearing caused 12 DOM mutations in the front tab and 0 in the hidden one, which then showed it within 1.5 s of being brought forward** (and a removal likewise).
- ★ **Idle tabs now park themselves** (the owner's autoclose): after **5 min** with no pointer/key/wheel/touch event, *no job of the user's in `scheduled`/`processing`*, and no `idleHold`, a tab shows a 30 s countdown banner (skipped when hidden — nobody to warn), then calls `window.close()` (works only for a script-opened tab) and otherwise **parks**: socket disconnected, `ParkedScreen` replacing the UI, one-click Resume ([App.jsx](client/src/App.jsx)). **Measured: live tab = 18 imgs / 22 videos / 288 card nodes → parked = 0 / 0 / 2, websocket closed, 0 frames received.** [idleHold.js](client/src/utils/idleHold.js) is the "someone is using this" flag — BookingDialog takes one while open, which is what stops a park (verified: form open, still unparked after 26 s on a 20 s test timer) *and* the auto-reload below.
- **Workflow switch now reloads the tab by itself** (the banner alone wasn't enough — the owner reported the refresh issue again): on a `workflow_info.id` change SocketContext reloads the page unless a form holds unsaved work, at most once per 60 s, and says why afterwards via sessionStorage. **Verified live: activating another workflow reloaded the open tab in 18 s, toasted "This machine now serves …", and the form came back rebuilt** (Ideogram's composer gone, MiniMax's video upload present). A form-open tab keeps the dismissible banner, which gained a **Reload** button.
- **The results grid mounts 60 cards at a time** (`CARD_PAGE`, +60 as the bottom comes into view, "Showing X of Y — load more") — hundreds of cards each with media is what made the grid slow to open ([Scheduler.jsx](client/src/pages/Scheduler.jsx)).
- ★★ **A restart always stops the machine serving — and that is BY DESIGN, not a bug.** [index.js](server/index.js) resolves the boot mode from a **one-shot flag** that `/activate-workflow` drops (`consumeStudentBootFlag()`), then persists it: *"Always start in admin… config.mode never decides the boot."* So nodemon, a crash, a power cut or a Windows update all bring the rig back in **admin mode**, where it answers HTTP but schedules nothing while every open student tab keeps reconnecting to it. (This is what made the rig look like it kept "falling back to admin" during this work — three times — and it cost a good hour of misdiagnosis: `GET /admin/mode` reads the persisted mode, which the boot has already rewritten to `admin`, so it looks identical to a failure. The failure path at [index.js](server/index.js) — `worker.start()` throwing → `c.mode = 'admin'` → exit — never actually fired.) **Owner chose: a rig resumes serving.** A second, *standing* marker `server/data/.serving` sits beside the one-shot `.boot-student` flag: `/activate-workflow` sets it, boot reads it (without consuming it), so a crash / nodemon / power cut / Windows update comes back **serving the same workflow**. It is cleared by the deliberate stops — **Reset to admin** and **emergency-stop** (`exitForRestart('admin')`) — and by a failed student boot, so a machine that *cannot* serve settles in admin instead of restarting forever; **Restart server** is neutral and keeps the machine doing what it was doing. ★ Both markers live in the gitignored `server/data/`, which means they DO travel in a cloned drive image (like the sqlite queue): a rig imaged from a serving machine will boot serving that workflow — delete `server/data/.serving` on the master image if that is not wanted. Two real fixes landed regardless: `worker.start()` is now retried **3× with 8 s gaps** before that fallback can fire, and — the genuine race it hid — [comfyProcess.js](server/workers/comfyProcess.js) no longer spawns a competing ComfyUI when one is merely *mid-boot*: `isApiResponsive()` allows just **1.5 s**, while a cold ComfyUI needs 30–90 s and holds the port long before it answers HTTP, so the new `isPortBound()` (`net.connect` probe) makes `start()` wait up to 90 s for the holder to come up (attach) or let go (spawn), and otherwise throw a plain-language error instead of a process that can never bind.
- **Still true / known limits:** the full snapshot on connect is ~370 KB per tab (it grows with `BROADCAST_JOB_LIMIT` = 500 jobs) and every tab reloading at once on a workflow switch pays it together — fine on a LAN, but if a workshop's history ever needs to be bigger, history should move to `GET /jobs` and out of the broadcast. Media is still served at full size (no thumbnails: that needs an image lib and `better-sqlite3` is deliberately the only native dep), so the first view of a 48 MB PiD upscale is still a 48 MB download — now cached afterwards.

## Latest progress (2026-09-17)

**Ideogram 4 — safety-filter false positives, visual prompt composer, Sage opt-out, default bbox fix (owner report, 2026-09-17)** — [image_ideogram4_t2i](workflows/image_ideogram4_t2i/). ★ **The gray "Image blocked by safety filter" picture is drawn by the model itself** (Ideogram's docs/prompting.md: *"False positive rates for safety is high for non-json prompts"*); ComfyUI's only "safety filter" string is in the paid API node. **Evidence on the rig:** 7 of the 26 Ideogram jobs in the DB came back blocked, incl. a harmless robot portrait in the correct schema; only 2 of 26 students' prompts used Ideogram's schema at all (the rest: plain French/English text, a bare quoted string, or invented JSON like `"subject"`/`"shot_type"`). **Measured (robot prompt, Turbo, 5 seeds, every image inspected by eye — a gray-pixel detector misses the half-blocked ones with ghost "Image bocked by sataty filter" text):** pretty-printed JSON (what ComfyQ sent) 2 fully gray / 1 partial / 2 clean → **compact JSON 0 gray** / 2 partial / 3 clean; same caption+seed on Quality: pretty = fully gray, compact = image. **The uncensored text encoder the Reddit post suggests (`Qwen3VL-8B-Uncensored-HauhauCS-Aggressive-Q4_K_M.gguf`, loads via ComfyUI-GGUF's `CLIPLoaderGGUF` type `ideogram4`) does NOT help:** compact+GGUF 2 fully gray / 3 clean, and on Quality it was fully gray where the official encoder rendered — the refusal is learned by the DiT from the conditioning, not a TE refusal — and it's 58% slower (22.4 s vs 14.2 s). **Not wired into the bundle** (core `CLIPLoader` can't list `.gguf`, which is why it wasn't selectable). Rewording body words ("skin", "chest") and CFG 5 didn't help either; a borderline prompt still needs another seed. ComfyUI's text wrapping matches the official pipeline (same chat template). **Fixes:**
- **New param field `format: "ideogram4-caption"`** ([schemas.js](server/config/schemas.js); round-trips through edit-data + editor save/preview like `linkedValues`): the worker compacts valid JSON (`JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(v))`, key order + non-ASCII kept) right before it goes into the graph ([promptFormat.js](server/workers/promptFormat.js), test in `test:storyboard`); plain text / broken JSON pass through unchanged. Verified end to end: a socket-booked pretty prompt reached ComfyUI with no newlines.
- **Visual composer** ([IdeogramComposer.jsx](client/src/components/capture/IdeogramComposer.jsx) + [ideogramCaption.js](client/src/utils/ideogramCaption.js)), shown by [DynamicParamFields](client/src/components/DynamicParamFields.jsx) under any `ideogram4-caption` textarea: a **Compose visually** button + a live status line (✓ caption / N format issues / plain text / invalid JSON). Full-screen portal: canvas shaped by the form's `aspect_ratio` param, drag to draw a box, drag to move, corner to resize, per-element colors; side panel for high-level description, background, Photo / Art style / No style, palettes (≤16 / ≤5, uppercase hex), elements (obj/text, text, desc, order, box on/off); checks against the documented schema (key order, required keys, bbox range, hex). Apply writes the caption indented (readable); the server compacts it. Plain text or other-shape JSON opens with that text as the high-level description (flattened), nothing is lost. **Written from Ideogram's schema — the linked d-daley/ideogram4-editor has no license, so none of its code is used.** Verified in headless Chrome against the Vite dev server (load 6 boxes, move, Cancel leaves the prompt, draw → text element → Apply → valid caption, reopen); it caught two bugs now fixed (stale model for one frame on reopen → the composer mounts per opening; the drawn box lost the last pointer move → tracked in a ref).
- **`requirements.disabledPerfFlags: ["use_sage_attention"]`** — Ideogram 4's DiT uses **256-wide attention heads**; `sageattention` 1.0.6 only accepts 64/96/128, so **every** DiT attention call raised and fell back to PyTorch (33,184 errors in one day's log). No speed lost: 14.2 s Turbo with and without Sage.
- **Default prompt bbox bug:** Ideogram's bbox is `[y_min, x_min, y_max, x_max]`, but ComfyUI's template example had elements 3/5/6 (STAY COZY banner, mini skateboard, bottom torn-paper strip) in `[x, y, …]` order — they rendered in the wrong place with garbled mirrored text. Swapped in meta default + api literal + template; rendered on 2 seeds: every element now lands where its description says, bottom text clean.
- ★★ **Follow-up, the real lever — BOUNDING BOXES (owner pointed at [this r/StableDiffusion post](https://www.reddit.com/r/StableDiffusion/comments/1tz4fnf/ideogram_4_a_solution_for_removing_the_annoying/), whose comments say "the fix is just to use enough bounding boxes"; measured here and it holds).** Same benign robot caption, same 5 seeds, only boxes added: **no box at all → blocked 5/5; one box on the subject → 0/5; 4 boxes → 0/5; 6 boxes → 0/5.** The owner's blocked jobs all had **one element with no bbox**. A matching-shape box also survived a seed that blocked every other variant (narrow box + tall cactus clean 2/2, while the same object in a wide box / full-frame box / no box was blocked at that seed). So: *give every element a box* — now warned about in `checkCaption`, hinted in the composer (element panel + empty state), and in the bundle description. The post's own two methods (first sigma +0.005; initial noise ×2, both LCM-only, two chained samplers) are **not reproducible on this rig**: they need `SamplerLCMCustom` (Extra Samplers), `NoiseMath` (More Math) and `Sigmas2 Mult` (RES4LYF) — only KJNodes' `CustomSigmas` is installed — and this ComfyUI's `KSamplerSelect` has no `lcm`. Left alone (the drive image is cloned to several rigs). Post comment worth knowing: the model **generates** the block picture (hence the garbled "Image bocked by sataty filter" variants), it doesn't swap in a fixed one; a LoRA at 0.15–0.25 for the first step reportedly removes it entirely.
- **Composer layout fidelity — verified by rendering test layouts and comparing against the drawn boxes** (4 corners / 4 edges / 3-in-a-row / single tall / single wide, at 1:1, 9:16, 16:9, 3:4, 21:9 and 0.25 / 1 / 2 MP): **the composer's coordinates are right** — four labelled corners landed in the right corners with the right colours at every aspect ratio and size, and placement is unaffected by resolution. Caveats found, all model behaviour rather than composer bugs, and now written into the composer's hints + the bundle description: (1) **box shape sets orientation** — a text element in a tall narrow box is rendered rotated 90°, sometimes upside down and garbled (the composer warns when a text box is >1.6× taller than wide); (2) **two boxes sitting apart in empty diagonal corners make Ideogram split the frame into separate panels** (a reproducible 2×2 collage with blank tiles — this is what looked like "elements repeating"), while adjacent boxes in a row render as one coherent scene, so keep boxes adjacent or add floor/wall boxes; (3) in **tall formats (9:16, 3:4) elements drift toward the middle** — a box at 76–94% height rendered at ~65%; (4) no repetition was reproducible from an over-large box (a wide box just placed a small object). **Output sizes at 1 MP:** 1024×1024 (1:1), 1376×768 (16:9), 768×1376 (9:16), 896×1184 (3:4), 1568×672 (21:9); 0.25 MP → 688×384 and 2 MP → 1936×1088 at 16:9.
- **Rig note:** several quick edits under `server/` restart ComfyQ repeatedly under nodemon; one student boot failed to start ComfyUI mid-race and **ComfyQ's fallback flipped the rig to admin mode** (it stops serving). Stage server edits and copy them in with one command, then check `/admin/mode`; re-serve with `POST /admin/activate-workflow`.

**Front-end stability + parameter recall (2026-09-17)** — owner report: the student UI sometimes hangs, drops, or recalls parameters wrong. **Root cause of the churn: every sampler progress tick (several per second, more on tiled/frame nodes) pushed the ENTIRE job list to every client**, and the 5 s heartbeat re-sent it even when nothing had changed — each one replacing all of `state`, so the whole grid, sidebar, timeline and the open booking form re-rendered.
- **Server:** `broadcast()` now coalesces bursts into ≤1 send per 250 ms and `_emitState()` **skips a state identical to the last one** (idle clients get nothing); a newcomer is sent the state directly on connect. **`queue.listRecent(500)`** replaces `list({ limit: 500 })` in the broadcast — `list` keeps the **OLDEST** rows, so past 500 jobs a workshop's new bookings would have vanished from every client; **`queue.listActive()`** (all unfinished jobs, unbounded) fixes the same bug in [statusSnapshot.js](server/federation/statusSnapshot.js), which reported an idle machine to ComfyQ Discovery while it was working. **No Discovery release needed** — `desktop/` is untouched and the snapshot shape is unchanged.
- **Client render path:** [mergeState.js](client/src/utils/mergeState.js) folds each broadcast into the previous state, reusing the identity of every job / `parameter_map` / `workflow_info` that didn't change (and returning `prev` when nothing did, which skips the render entirely); the new memoized [JobCard.jsx](client/src/components/JobCard.jsx) then re-renders only the card whose job changed. Scheduler memoizes its filter+sort and passes stable callbacks; MyJobsPanel memoizes its list; the timeline's removal diff is Set-based (was O(n²) per broadcast). **Measured in headless Chrome against the dev server: 57 cards, 0 DOM mutations over two 6 s windows while idle** (48 before settling = first paint).
- **Booking/recall:** the form is filled on opening **and** when the `parameter_map` arrives later (a dialog opened before the first broadcast stayed empty) or the served workflow changes, guarded by a `filledFor` ref so nothing typed is ever wiped; the time mode resets per opening (a stale "Pick a time" used to persist); a recalled/default dropdown value the workflow no longer offers is snapped to a real option (it used to display option 1 while submitting something else); **a reused media file is HEAD-checked** and dropped with a note if it is no longer on the machine (storyboard chained frames are swept after a day); previews use `URL.createObjectURL` + revoke instead of `readAsDataURL` (a phone video no longer blocks the tab and sits in memory as base64); a `submitting` ref prevents a double booking. Verified live: prompt + seed + dropdowns recalled through "Use these settings", typed text intact after 12 s idle, reopening gives a fresh default form.
- **Workflow switches are now announced to open tabs (owner report: "with several tabs open you had to close and reopen the tab for job planning to work").** Activating another workflow restarts the server, so every tab reconnects holding a form built for the *previous* workflow — its parameter keys mean nothing to the new graph. SocketContext now watches `workflow_info.id`, and on a change raises a toast + the dismissible `WorkflowSwitchBanner` ([App.jsx](client/src/App.jsx)); BookingDialog rebuilds its form for the new workflow (the `filledFor` ref) and says so inline if it was open at the time; Scheduler drops a stale "Use these settings" prefill. **Verified live with a tab left open:** switching to Krea 2 raised the banner **18 s** later, the card and the booking form rebuilt themselves (Krea's LoRA fields, composer button gone), and switching back restored Ideogram's form + composer — **no page reload at any point**.
- **Media/UX:** [useInView.js](client/src/hooks/useInView.js) — card videos play only while on screen (**verified: 0 of 9 playing at the top of the grid, 4 after scrolling**; all 9 autoplayed before) and an inline `ModelViewer` mounts only when visible, so scrolling past many 3D results can't exhaust the browser's ~16 WebGL contexts (same hook, but this rig's history has no GLB job to exercise it). `bookJob` waits for the server's ack and **refuses while offline** so socket.io can't replay a buffered booking twice; BookingDialog keeps the form (and its uploads) on a refusal; a **reconnecting banner** in [App.jsx](client/src/App.jsx) replaces silently stale data; Modal's body-scroll lock is ref-counted (overlapping modals left the page locked/unlocked wrongly); Toast timers no longer restart on every parent render.

## Latest progress (2026-09-15)

**Krea 2 Turbo t2i fixed — stray text + mixed LoRA styles; enhance / thinking / LoRA toggles (owner report, 2026-09-15)** — [image_krea2_turbo_t2i_lora](workflows/image_krea2_turbo_t2i_lora/). ★ **Root cause, measured on the rig by tapping the prompt that reaches `CLIPTextEncode`:** the Comfy template's enhancer system prompt says *"Think step by step… consider two or three alternative styles… write one paragraph after the thinking block"* while `TextGenerate.thinking` is **false** (and ComfyUI's base `TextGenerate` never strips a think block) — so Qwen3-VL-4B wrote its **whole plan** ("Step 1: Identify subject… Step 9… Final output:") as the answer, truncated by `max_length 512` **before the real description even started**, and Krea 2 rendered that: stray words (a newspaper labelled "newsper heebtspier") and the plan's chosen style ("realistic photograph") mixed with the LoRA's. On top of that the LoRA **trigger words were blank** (the flattened graph lost the template's CustomCombo mapping), so style LoRAs barely engaged. **Probe (36 enhancer runs, 6 prompts):** thinking **off** leaked planning 1–3/6 regardless of system prompt, returned **empty** text, and once **refused** ("I'm sorry, but I can't fulfill that request." → an unrelated portrait); thinking **on** gave 0 leaks / 0 text mentions in 18/18 and is faster (~9 s vs up to 36 s) — this 4B model emits **no `<think>` content at all**; the flag only changes the chat template. **Fix (API graph; template got the rewritten system prompt only):** (1) two rewritten system prompts — silent planning, "output only the prompt", never mention text unless asked — switched by the LoRA toggle (`9920` + `ComfySwitchNode 9921`): with a style LoRA on, the enhancer is told **not to name any style or medium**; (2) `TextGenerate.thinking` **on by default**; (3) cleanup chain `9922` strip `^.*</think>` → `9923` strip a leading "Final prompt:" label → `9924` trim → `9925 RegexMatch` (non-empty and not a refusal) → `9926` switch **falls back to the student's own words**; (4) **automatic trigger words** via the new generic `linkedValues` (below) — 14 LoRAs, verified: Comfy-Org/Krea-2 README (current + commit a2dc601 for coolblue/plasmoid/warmpastel, recommended 0.8), `ilkerzgi/krea-2-embroidered-textile-diorama-lora` card, nos_invisibles' safetensors `ss_tag_frequency`, realism (gokaygokay) needs none; the dropdown shows each style's trigger. Student form now: **Prompt · Enhance prompt with AI · Thinking mode · Aspect ratio · Use a style LoRA · Style LoRA · strength · Seed**, with thinking greyed out when enhancing is off and the LoRA picker + strength greyed out when the LoRA is off. Before/after on the same seeds: darkbrush now a real monochrome ink wash, retro-anime a purple retro anime, no stray words; recalibrated **10 s warm / 14 s cold**.
- **New generic param field `linkedValues`** ([schemas.js](server/config/schemas.js)): `[{ nodeId, field, map, fallback? }]` — the param's chosen value also writes other node fields (`map[value]`, else `fallback`; no fallback = leave the literal). Applied in `_materializeWorkflow` **before** the exposed params (an exposed param on the same field wins) and falls back to the param's default when unsent; round-trips through the editor like `disabledWhen`; `lora` dropdown labels append a linked string value as a hint. Tests: [linkedValues.test.js](server/workers/linkedValues.test.js) (in `test:storyboard`).
- **`disabledWhen` now works on every field type** — selects/LoRA pickers, checkboxes and text/number inputs grey out with a "not used; change X to use it" note ([DynamicParamFields.jsx](client/src/components/DynamicParamFields.jsx)); before, only textareas and media honoured it.
- **Editor order bug fixed:** `GET /admin/workflows/:id/edit-data` returned params in **graph order**, and the editor saves `order` from list position — so the Student preview didn't match the real form and **any admin re-save silently reordered a workflow's fields**. Exposed params now come first in their saved order ([admin.js](server/routes/admin.js)).
- **Rig note:** editing files under `server/` restarts ComfyQ under nodemon, and that restart can take a ComfyUI **owned by ComfyQ** down with it (it did once here, mid-probe) — relaunch via `POST /admin/comfyui/launch`.

`CannyEdgePreprocessor` 92/200 and `DWPreprocessor` body+hands+face / `yolox_l.onnx` / `dw-ll_ucoco_384_bs5.torchscript.pt` unwired, with the author's note "connect Canny or Pose into the scale-to-multiple Resize") — built exactly that way, in api **and** template, by a script (template→API round-trip diffs = only the annotator swap). ★ **Annotator resolution trap:** controlnet_aux annotators return frames at their own `resolution` (512), which silently overrode "Working resolution" → the API graph drives the source resize **and** the annotator `resolution` from one `PrimitiveInt 9910` (exposed). ★ **ref0.5 = a 64-px grid:** the IC-LoRA's `latent_downscale_factor` makes the scale-to-multiple step 64, so a working resolution of 544 rendered at 512 → **1024p**, not the "≈1088p" the depth bundle's description claimed — all three now expose it in **steps of 64, default 512 (→ 1024p)** (depth bundle meta fixed; its api literal untouched). **Pose uses the TorchScript detector `yolox_l.torchscript.pt`, not the candidate's `yolox_l.onnx`** (owner follow-up): ★ this rig's `python_embeded` has **both `onnxruntime` 1.24.1 (CPU) and `onnxruntime-gpu` 1.22.0 installed** — they share one `onnxruntime/` package dir and the later CPU install overwrote the core DLL/pyd (only a stale `onnxruntime_providers_cuda.dll` from the GPU wheel remains), so `get_available_providers()` is CPU-only and controlnet_aux silently falls back to **OpenCV-on-CPU** for `.onnx` detectors. Measured on 125 frames @512: DWPose **64.5 s** with `yolox_l.onnx` vs **5.1 s** with the TorchScript detector (GPU via torch), identical pose maps (mean abs diff 0.04/255) → pose bundle warm **133 s → 83 s**. The 217 MB `yolox_l.torchscript.pt` auto-downloaded to `custom_nodes/comfyui_controlnet_aux/ckpts/hr16/yolox-onnx/`. The environment itself was **not** changed (the drive image is cloned to several rigs): the only other ONNX users among the bundles are LivePortrait's landmark runner and rembg (TRELLIS.2 background removal); repairing it = uninstall both packages, reinstall `onnxruntime-gpu` alone, verify CUDA EP on Blackwell — and any custom-node install that pulls plain `onnxruntime` breaks it again. **Style finding:** with canny edges a watercolor prompt stayed photographic (twice), claymation took over completely → canny defaults to the tested claymation prompt and its description says strong physical looks work best. All three now calibrate with the start image OFF (`warmupParams`), since the calibrator otherwise stages a random picture as the first frame.

LTX 2.5 equivalents exist for all of them. Candidate JSONs stay in `_candidate_workflows/`. Still installed from LTX 2.3: `video_edit_ltx2_3_3dreal_vid2vid`, `video_ltx2_3_ia2v_iclora`, `video_ltx2_3_ic_lora_ingredients`.

To keep exact sizes, [video_ltx2_5_i2v](workflows/video_ltx2_5_i2v/) swapped its top-level `ResolutionSelector` (aspect + megapixels) for **Width/Height `PrimitiveInt` 410/411** (512–1920, step 64, default 1280×704) in api and template. **Measured on the rig:** both bundles render **1920×1088 exactly** (a 1080 request snaps to 1088; the old flf2v's 1056 floor is gone), take **whole seconds at 24 fps → `s×24+1` frames** (5 s = 121 frames = 5.04 s; 6 s = 145), and cost **127 s (i2v) / 202 s (flf2v)** at 1920×1088 × 5 s — ~3× their calibrated estimates. ## Earlier progress (2026-09-14, morning)

**All 28 `_candidate_workflows` processed → 26 new EXPERIMENTAL bundles (25 after the owner removed the LTX-2 depth v2v), each built, run on the 5090 and calibrated (2026-09-14)** — owner asked for every staged candidate to become a ComfyQ bundle (api + meta + template + runtime), marked **experimental** so they can be tested by hand and validated from the admin panel. Skipped by owner decision: `video_minimax_h3_t2v_turbo_4step_working` (same graph as the production `video_minimax_h3_t2v_turbo`). The candidate JSONs were **left in `_candidate_workflows/`** (owner decision — delete once validated). Bundles: `3d_pixal3d_multi_views`, `3d_pixal3d_trellis2_image_to_model`, `image_edit_krea2_dynamic_character_sheet`, `image_edit_krea2_quadview`, `utility_pid_image_upscale` (replaces the two t2i PiD candidates — see the PiD entry below), `utility_ltx2_5_video_deblur_upscale` (the "LTX 2.5 v2v single stage" candidate is really the Deblur/2× pixel-upscaler IC-LoRA), `utility_marigold_v2_depth`, `utility_marigold_v2_normals` (**both removed 2026-10-05**), `utility_seedvr2_3b_int8_video_upscale`, `utility_seedvr2_7b_int8_image_upscale`, `video_edit_ltx2_5_iclora_union_control`, `video_edit_ltx2_v2v_canny_control` + `…depth_control` (LTX-2 19B — **both removed by the owner**, replaced by the LTX 2.5 IC-LoRA depth/canny/pose trio, see the entry above), `video_edit_minimax_h3_fun_controlnet_{canny,depth,union}`, `video_edit_minimax_h3_v2v_video_reference`, `video_edit_wan21_scail2_character_replacement`, `video_edit_wan_animate2`, `video_edit_wan_idv2v_restyle_{compare,hires}`, `video_ltx2_3_ic_lora_ingredients`, `video_ltx2_5_{flf2v,i2v,t2v}`, `video_minimax_h3_i2v_4step`.

**PiD reworked into an image-upscale utility (owner follow-up, 2026-09-14)** — the two PiD candidates were Flux.2 Klein 9B / Krea 2 Turbo *text-to-image* + PiD graphs; the owner wants PiD as **upload an image → PiD**, filed under **Utilities**, and does **not** want the generation variants — so `image_flux2_klein_9b_tiled_pid_33mp` + `image_krea2_turbo_tiled_pid_33mp` were **deleted** and replaced by [utility_pid_image_upscale](workflows/utility_pid_image_upscale/) (`category` **i2i**). Graph (8 nodes, all core + `ComfyUI-Latent-Tiled-PiD`): `LoadImage` → `VAEEncode` (**qwen_image_vae**) → `LatentTiledPiDDecode` (`pid_1.5_qwenimage_1024_to_4096_4step_bf16`, `latent_format qwenimage`, Gemma `pixeldit` empty prompt, `max_tile 1024` / `overlap 64` / `degrade_sigma 0`) → `SaveImage`. PiD decodes a *latent*, so an uploaded image only needs a VAE encode in front; **output = exactly 4× the upload** (the upload box caps it at 1920×1080 → 7680×4320, 33 MP, 2×2 tiles). Exposes **image + seed** only. ★ **Why the Qwen lane, not FLUX.2 — A/B on the rig** (phone photo / generated render / compressed JPEG, colour compared after blurring away texture): Qwen mean ΔE **1.2 / 1.3 / 1.7** vs FLUX.2 **7.1 / 1.6 / 2.2** — the FLUX.2 lane visibly shifted a tan shirt pink on the phone photo — and Qwen synthesizes more real detail; its weakness is **inventing texture on poor inputs** (grime on a flat board, cartoonish tiny faces in a messaging-app JPEG), which the student description warns about. `degrade_sigma 0.3` re-drew content and drifted colour on both → left at 0. Verified: 1920×1080 → 7680×4320 in **18 s** (3 warm runs, and 18 s after `/free` — the 8 GB of models reload from RAM), no seam at the tile cross, ~48 MB PNG, ComfyQ's collector returns the one image. **★ Calibration caveat:** the automatic split reported 8 s warm because PiD's progress bar counts **tiles** — the first tile is billed as model load — so the bundle's `runtime.json` holds the hand-measured 18 s (`source: "manual"` + a note); **re-calibrating overwrites it with the underestimate**. Template generated from the api.json through ComfyUI's own frontend (`app.loadApiJson` → `graph.serialize()`, + a Note) and round-tripped back through `convert.mjs`: identical graph. The PiD candidate JSONs stay in `_candidate_workflows/`.

**How they were built (repeatable):** (1) **UI→API via ComfyUI's own frontend** — [tools/ui-to-api/convert.mjs](tools/ui-to-api/convert.mjs) loads each UI graph in headless Chrome and calls `app.graphToPrompt()` (the "Export (API)" code path), so subgraphs/bypass/primitives flatten exactly as ComfyUI does. Validated by converting 4 production templates and diffing with their hand-made api.json: identical except the documented hand edits. (2) Parallel agents drafted a meta + pruning plan per workflow (final-result outputs only — owner decision; in-path `PreviewAny`/`PreviewImage`/`GetMeshInfo` passthroughs rewired, not deleted); every draft was reviewed and machine-validated (every exposed param → an existing literal field, select options ⊆ the live combo list, number bounds inside the schema, combo/model values installed, links intact). (3) Each bundle ran through the real `POST /workflows/:id/calibrate`, then ComfyQ's own output collector was run on the ComfyUI history and **the output content was checked** (pixel stats / sampled video frames / GLB magic) — "success" alone hid black images. Warm times on the 5090 range 1 s (Marigold) to 322 s (MiniMax video-reference, 20-step default).

**Candidate bugs found and fixed (API graph; the first two also patched into the bundle template):** MiniMax **canny + depth** had `GetVideoComponents` 709 **bypassed** in the live control chain → the converter dropped the link → Canny/depth had **no input** (ComfyUI would reject the prompt; the candidates could never have run as saved) — un-bypassed. **Pixal3D multi-view**: all four `ImageCropToMask` read the **front** image (only the RemoveBackground inputs had been re-wired to the left/back/right loaders) — fixed. **LTX 2.5 deblur**: 544-px short side → 17-px latent that the pixel-upscaler IC-LoRA patchifies 2×2 → `can't divide axis of length 17` — added a "scale to multiple (factor×32)" resize like its union-control sibling; output-size param now 256–1088 step 64. **API-only improvements (template NOT updated — re-export would lose them):** SCAIL-2's Extend segment re-wired to the Base segment's prompt/SAM3 text/mode/fast/size controls (else a student's edits only affected the first ~3 s); `Video Slice` trims with an exposed clip length on MiniMax canny/depth/pose/v2v and LTX 2.5 union/deblur (they decoded the whole upload into RAM); an `ImageScale` before Canny (MiniMax); MiniMax i2v output follows the image aspect (the candidate stretched it into a square); Krea2 candidates re-pointed at the on-disk renames (`Krea2_Turbo_convrot_int8mixed`, `DynamicCharacterSheet_krea2_v1`, `QuadView_krea2_v1`; template patched too).

**New infra (generic, all verified):**
- **Experimental flag** — `meta.experimental` (zod, default false) → `summary.experimental`; the admin library ([WorkflowSelector.jsx](client/src/components/WorkflowSelector.jsx)) shows an amber **Experimental** badge, an **Experimental N** filter chip, and a per-card **Validate** button → confirm modal ([AdminConfig.jsx](client/src/pages/AdminConfig.jsx)) → **`PUT /admin/workflows/:id/experimental {experimental}`** ([admin.js](server/routes/admin.js)), which rewrites that one key **in meta.json** (not config.meta.json, so the sign-off is committed with the bundle; `false` deletes the key). The meta editor has a Status checkbox, and **`PUT /admin/workflows/:id/meta` keeps the stored flag when a payload omits it** (an older client can't silently validate by re-saving).
- **Per-workflow perf-flag opt-out — `requirements.disabledPerfFlags`** ([perfFlags.js](server/workers/perfFlags.js)). ★ **Verified on this rig:** the global **Sage attention** flag makes **Qwen-Image-Edit (2509 and 2511) save all-black images** — which silently broke the PRODUCTION `image_edit_qwen_multiple_scene_angles` + `…character_angles` since Sage was enabled 2026-08-30 — and Marigold (built on it); **`--fast fp16_accumulation`** makes **SeedVR2 7B int8** save black. Neither crashes, so the existing sage-crash recovery never fired. Those 5 bundles now declare the flags they can't run with; since ComfyQ serves one workflow at a time, **student boot** ([index.js](server/index.js)) launches ComfyUI without them and **replaces an already-running ComfyUI that has them** (`worker.ensureNoBlockingPerfFlags()`, reads `/system_stats` argv), and **`AdminCalibrator.calibrate(id)`** runs with exactly that workflow's flags (restarts on flag changes; a spawn masked for a workflow is not bounced by a generic launch / Open in ComfyUI). Every other workflow keeps the speed-ups. Verified end to end: flagged external ComfyUI → student boot for Marigold killed it, spawned without Sage, a booked job returned a real depth map.
- **Output collector fixes** ([outputCollector.js](server/executor/outputCollector.js)): records with `type:'input'` are skipped (ComfyUI 0.35's core **LoadVideo reports the uploaded clip as a UI preview** → a broken extra tile in EVERY video-input job, production included); and a record carrying both `filename` and an absolute `fullpath` (**VHS_VideoCombine**) is no longer collected twice (LivePortrait was affected).
- **Named calibration assets** — a `warmupParams` media value of **`asset:<file in the assets dir>`** stages that exact file (pose / character-replacement / restyle workflows need a person, not "the smallest video"). The new bundles use the dancer clip `2795750-uhd…mp4` + its clay-restyled frame `Flux2-Klein_00003_.png`.
- **Friendly error:** silent source video in a workflow that keeps the soundtrack → "Your video has no sound track…" (`NO_AUDIO_MESSAGE`, [errorMessages.js](server/executor/errorMessages.js)).

**Still to judge by hand (not failures):** ~~ID-V2V bundles need the local ComfyUI PR #15139 patch (still unmerged 2026-09-14; tag `requires-idv2v-patch`)~~ — ⚠ **superseded: the PR merged upstream as `5c4d2568` on 2026-09-27, so both bundles run on a stock ComfyUI ≥ 0.39.x and the tag has been removed** (verified 2026-10-07, see the sweep entry at the top and [docs/comfyui-idv2v.md](docs/comfyui-idv2v.md)); MiniMax pose (union) rendered a literal stick figure with the calibration prompt — check with a real prompt; the four MiniMax ref2v graphs default Fast mode **off** (author's choice, turbo LoRA v0.1); the 3D bundles default to 700k faces + 4096² PBR maps → ~60 MB `.glb`; the dynamic character-sheet's LLM system prompt text looks partly corrupted in the candidate (it still works); the storyboard alias test now matches any workflow count. **Side note:** `comfyui-workflow-encrypt` re-exports ComfyUI's global `NODE_CLASS_MAPPINGS`, so `/object_info` attributes VHS/KJ/LTXVideo/PiD nodes to it — harmless, but it skews the model audit's `rescan_nodes.py` pack attribution.

## Earlier progress (2026-08-30)

**MiniMax H3 4-step turbo t2v + opt-in ComfyUI perf flags (2026-08-30)** — [video_minimax_h3_t2v_turbo](workflows/video_minimax_h3_t2v_turbo/), the first **MiniMax H3** bundle, built to answer "make H3 faster on this rig". **Measured 146.8 s → 34.2 s** for the same 5 s 848×480 clip with audio (**4.3×**), all numbers taken on the rig (RTX 5090) by POSTing the real graph to ComfyUI, not estimated. **Text → video *with a matching audio track*** (H3 is omni-modal: one sampler pass produces both, decoded by two VAEs — `VAEDecode` + `VAEDecodeAudio` — and muxed by `CreateVideo`). **All comfy-core** — H3 lives in `comfy_extras/nodes_minimax_h3.py` as of ComfyUI 0.34.0, so **no custom-node dep**. `category` **i2v** (t2v is generation, per the taxonomy rule); `minVRAM 24`.

**Where the speed comes from (ranked, measured):** (1) **the 4-step turbo LoRA is the whole story — 3.8× on its own** (`minimax_h3_fl2v_turbo_4step_v1.0_768p_comfyui_bf16.safetensors`, 1.96 GB, from `Comfy-Org/MiniMax-H3` — the same repo as the H3 models; it was simply **not downloaded**, the rig had ~110 LoRAs but zero for H3). (2) **Resolution ≈ the pixel ratio**: 1344×768 costs **2.6×** vs 848×480 (90.5 s vs 34.2 s) — 1344×768 is what the LoRA was distilled at (the "768p" in its name), 848×480 is the classroom default. (3) **The perf flags add ~11%.** (4) **Cold vs warm ≈ 50 s** — the first job after a ComfyUI start pays the 20 GB transformer + 15 GB text-encoder load (35 GB against a 32 GB card, so they can't both stay resident); in a queue only the first student pays it. **★ CFG is already 1** — H3 uses `BasicGuider` with no negative branch, so there is no second forward per step to remove; don't go looking for that lever.

**New: two opt-in ComfyUI performance flags**, both **off by default** so every existing rig keeps byte-identical behavior — `comfy_ui.use_sage_attention` → `--use-sage-attention`, and `comfy_ui.fp16_accumulation` → **`--fast fp16_accumulation`** (*only* that one feature; **never bare `--fast`**, which enables everything in ComfyUI's `PerformanceFeature` enum that ComfyUI itself labels "untested and potentially quality deteriorating"). Threaded through [schemas.js](server/config/schemas.js) → [configManager.js](server/config/configManager.js) → `PUT /admin/comfy` ([admin.js](server/routes/admin.js)) → [localComfyUIWorker.js](server/workers/localComfyUIWorker.js) → [comfyProcess.js](server/workers/comfyProcess.js), with two checkboxes under **Admin → ComfyUI backend → Performance** ([AdminConfig.jsx](client/src/pages/AdminConfig.jsx)). **★ They are part of `AdminCalibrator._comfySig()`**, so toggling one makes the next launch/Restart actually respawn ComfyUI instead of reusing the old process — the same mechanism that already restarts on a path change. **Rig note:** `sageattention` 1.0.6 + `triton` 3.4.0 were already installed and **verified working on Blackwell (sm_120)** — ComfyQ had simply never passed the flag; both are now **ON** on this rig. Being global, they're toggles, not hardcoded args: if quality or stability ever looks off, turn them off + restart to rule them out.

**Bundle build notes:** the api.json was **hand-built from `/object_info` schemas** rather than converted from the litegraph template — ComfyUI's own `video_minimax_h3_t2v` template is a **subgraph** workflow, and flattening subgraph→API by hand is error-prone (positional `widgets_values`, autogrow inputs like `ComfyMathExpression.values.a`). Building 20 clean nodes against the live schemas and then **running it on ComfyUI** is both the validation and the benchmark. Topology mirrors the official template, including its **Fast-mode pattern** (`PrimitiveBoolean` → two `ComfySwitchNode`s swapping model base↔turbo-LoRA and steps 20↔4) — the same shape as the Flux.2 bundles. Dropped the template's `ComfyMathExpression`+`PrimitiveFloat` seconds→frames pair in favour of exposing `length` directly (the node snaps to its own 17k+5 grid anyway). Exposes **prompt / Fast mode / width / height / length / seed** (6 params); `estimatedDurationSec: 35` matches the measured warm time. The **`_template.json` is ComfyUI's official template patched** (Fast mode on, 4-step LoRA, steps 4, 848×480) so "Open in ComfyUI" still hands over an editable subgraph — the template is display/round-trip only, so its node ids need not match the flat api.json. Validated: 20/20 nodes reachable from the single `SaveVideo`, no preview sinks, all 6 params resolve to real settable literals, and a direct `_materializeWorkflow` test confirms injection incl. **`false` surviving** for the Fast-mode toggle (the materializer skips `''`/null/undefined, not `false`).

**★ Staging-folder gotcha:** a `minimax_h3_t2v_turbo.json` already sat in the owner's `Downloads/workflows_a_tester/` but **cannot run on this rig** — it needs `MiniMaxH3TurboLoRA` + `MiniMaxH3TurboSampler` (a custom pack that isn't installed) and names three models that don't exist here (`..._fl2va_int8_convrot` vs the rig's `..._fl2va_**pruned**_int8_convrot`, `qwen3vl_32b_minimax_h3_int8_convrot` vs `..._**nvfp4_awq**`, and a `minimax_h3_turbo_4step_ema_ckpt500` that was never present). Left untouched; a working `minimax_h3_t2v_turbo_4step_WORKING.json` was added beside it and `_SETUP_NOTES.md` documents the difference.

**Discovery — wider default scan range + a "Password required" lock chip (2026-08-30)** — two changes to the **ComfyQ – Discovery** desktop app, shipped together as a release. (1) **`DEFAULT_EXTRA_SCAN` widened from `['10.10.16.0/23']` to `['10.10.14-18.1-254']`** ([main.js](desktop/main.js)) — the rigs no longer all sit on the old `10.10.16.0/23`, so every install now sweeps **10.10.14.1 → 10.10.18.254** (1270 probes) on top of its own local subnet(s). Well under `MAX_SCAN_HOSTS` (4096); at 48-way concurrency / 1.5 s timeout a sweep is ~40 s, inside the 60 s `SCAN_MS` cadence — and `scanRange_sweep()` already skips a tick while one is running, so an overrun is harmless. The `parseRangeSpec()` `a.b.c-d.e-f` form was verified to expand to exactly that range. Per-machine overrides (`COMFYQ_FED_SCAN`, `scanRanges` in `fleet-config.json`, the in-app Search-range field) are unchanged and still *additional*. (2) **Lock chip** — the card now renders an amber **🔒 Password required** chip (new `lock` glyph in `ICON` + `LOCK_SVG`) when the peer's snapshot says `accessLocked`, and the Schedule button gains a 🔒 + tooltip; `accessLocked` was added to `cardsSignature()` so a machine being locked actually triggers a card rebuild (the renderer only rebuilds on a signature change — see the 2026-06-28 d entry). **Backward compatible:** an older server omits the field entirely, which reads as open. Verified by running the real `cardHtml()` in a stubbed-DOM vm across locked / open / field-absent peers. See the access-password entry above for where `accessLocked` comes from.

**Optional student access password — reserving a machine for one group (2026-08-30)** — a rig can now be **locked with a password students must enter before they can connect to it**, so an admin can serve a workflow while keeping the GPU for one class. Fully optional: **no password set = today's behavior, byte for byte** (every gate short-circuits on an empty hash). It is a **second, independent password** — `config.auth.accessPasswordHash` alongside the existing `adminPasswordHash` (which still gates destructive/admin actions and is unchanged); the two are set in separate cards and neither implies the other. Admin UI: a **"Student access"** card in [AdminConfig.jsx](client/src/pages/AdminConfig.jsx) (`Lock`/`LockOpen` + a *Password required* / *Open to everyone* badge) → **`PUT /admin/access-password`** ([admin.js](server/routes/admin.js), `adminGate`d, empty string = reopen); `GET /admin/config` grew `hasAccessPassword` and now strips **both** hashes. Student side: [App.jsx](client/src/App.jsx) checks **`GET /access/status`** before mounting `SocketProvider` and shows the new [AccessGate.jsx](client/src/components/AccessGate.jsx) ("This machine is reserved") instead of the student UI; **`/admin` is never behind the gate** so an admin can always get back in to lift it. **★ Token, not plaintext:** `POST /access/login` exchanges the password for a token stored in localStorage ([access.js](client/src/utils/access.js)) and replayed on the socket handshake + REST calls — the plaintext is never stored client-side. The token is **derived deterministically from the bcrypt hash** (`sha256('comfyq-access-v1:'+hash)`, [authGate.js](server/auth/authGate.js)), which is what makes it practical: verifying is a cheap constant-time compare rather than a ~100 ms bcrypt per socket handshake/upload, tokens survive a server restart with no session table, and **changing or clearing the password rotates the hash → every issued token dies at once**. Three enforcement points, all no-ops when unlocked: (1) a **Socket.IO `io.use()` handshake gate** in [realtimeBus.js](server/realtime/realtimeBus.js) — this is the one that actually stops generation, since booking rides the socket; (2) `accessGate` express middleware on **`/jobs` + the upload routes** in [index.js](server/index.js); (3) **`bus.enforceAccess()`**, called right after the password changes, which re-verifies every live socket and disconnects the stale ones (emitting `access_revoked`) — otherwise locking a machine mid-session would leave whoever was already on it still able to book. The client treats both `access_revoked` and a refused handshake (`connect_error` → `accessRequired`) as "drop the token, show the gate again", so a lost revoke packet still converges via the reconnect attempt. The gate also **accepts a valid admin password**, so the admin panel keeps working on a locked machine. **★ Header vs. query:** the token normally rides the `X-Access-Token` header, but browser-issued GETs can't set headers — so `tokenFromRequest()` also reads `?access_token=`, and `getInputUrl`/`getIngredientsUrl` ([api.js](client/src/utils/api.js)) append it via `withAccessToken()` (the "Use these settings" media previews and the ingredients-zip `<a href>` would otherwise 401 on a locked rig). **Deliberately NOT gated:** media serving (`/images`, `/download`) — those URLs are only discoverable through the gated socket, and gating them would break every `<img>`/`<video>` tag; and `/workflows` — the admin library fetches it in student mode without the token. **Also needed:** `'/access'` added to the **Vite dev proxy** ([vite.config.js](client/vite.config.js)) — the proxy whitelists backend prefixes explicitly, so the handshake 404s in dev without it. Federation: `buildSnapshot()` gained an **`accessLocked`** boolean so a fleet monitor can show a lock instead of an open "Schedule a job" (the Discovery app's UI isn't wired to it yet). Verified with a 24-assertion end-to-end test against the real `RealtimeBus`/`authGate` (open → lock → refuse socket+REST → kick live sockets → login → token works via header *and* query → rotate invalidates the old token → admin override → unlock reopens everything), plus a live check against the running dev server; client build green. **No rate limiting on `/access/login`** — bcrypt's ~100 ms cost is the only throttle, matching the existing admin password (fine for a classroom, not for the open internet).

**Wan 2.2 key-frame animation — 4 frames → video (2026-07-06)** — [video_wan22_4_key_frames](workflows/video_wan22_4_key_frames/), a **fully verbatim** `_candidate_workflows/` promotion (zero graph edits — no preview sinks to prune). **Key-frame → video *generation*** (a multi-keyframe sibling of `video_ltx2_3_flf2v` (**⚠ removed 2026-09-14** — superseded by [video_ltx2_5_flf2v](workflows/video_ltx2_5_flf2v/))): upload **4 still key frames** (`62/122/124/126 LoadImage`) and Wan 2.2 runs **3 chained FLF2V segments** (`WanFirstLastFrameToVideo`, frame 1→2 / 2→3 / 3→4), each a **dual high/low-noise** sampler pass on the 4-step Lightning LoRAs; the decoded frames of all 3 segments are concatenated (`133/134/135 ImageBatch`) + the last key frame appended → `145 CreateVideo` → `146 SaveVideo`. **`category` i2v (Video generation), NOT video-edit** — it grows a *new* video from images (the "i2v is not an edit" rule holds; a multi-image input doesn't make it an edit). Exposes (12 params) **4 key-frame images / 3 per-transition motion prompts (`140:6`,`228:225`,`245:242` — one per segment, optional; empty = pure morph) / global Width + Height / 3 per-segment high-noise seeds (`140:57`,`228:222`,`245:239`)**. **Global resolution (owner follow-up 2026-07-07):** the owner added two shared `PrimitiveInt` nodes — Width `283` + Height `284` — wired into all three FLF2V segments' `width`/`height` in **both** the `_template.json` (editable graph) **and** the executable `.api.json`, so **one Width + one Height param drives every segment at once** (they must stay equal across segments — the three feed a shared `ImageBatch`, which is exactly why a single shared source, not per-segment fields, is the right design). Exposed as `number` (default 720×720, 256–1280 step 16); the api/template PrimitiveInt literals shipped as `0` (a broken placeholder → 0×0) and were set to **720** (the graph's original resolution). Length stays 25 frames/segment; the Chinese quality negatives + 4-step/cfg-1 sampler settings stay fixed. **★ Sync note:** editing a `_template.json` alone does nothing at runtime — ComfyQ executes the `.api.json`; the same node-add + rewire had to be mirrored there (here the owner did both, and the meta had to expose `283.value`/`284.value`). **All comfy-core** — no custom-node deps. 6 Wan 2.2 models (`umt5_xxl_fp8` CLIP + high/low-noise 14B fp8 UNETs + two lightx2v 4-step LoRAs + `wan_2.1_vae`), **all on the rig**; each segment loads the same set but ComfyUI caches by filename → one set in VRAM, `minVRAM 16` (dual 14B fp8, like the Bernini Wan bundles). Calibration cache-immune (3 high-noise `noise_seed` literals → `_randomizeSeeds`; 4 image inputs auto-resolve from `assets.dir`). Template copied **verbatim** (openable in ComfyUI), canonical hyphenated `<id>` kept. 54 nodes; the 3 unreachable-from-`SaveVideo` nodes (`140:60`/`228:214`/`245:231`, per-segment `CreateVideo`s) are inert leftovers — not OUTPUT_NODEs, never executed. Validated off-rig (registry + `hasTemplate`, all 12 params → real settable node/field, orders 0–11 unique, Width/Height drive all 3 FLF2V nodes, one SaveVideo & no preview sinks, seed literals, candidate hidden). Pending rig verification.

**LTX 2.3 3DReal — rough 3D render → photoreal vid2vid (2026-07-06)** — [video_edit_ltx2_3_3dreal_vid2vid](workflows/video_edit_ltx2_3_3dreal_vid2vid/), the **3DReal sibling** of `video_edit_ltx2_3_ic_lora_vid2vid` (⚠ removed 2026-09-14) and the **first bundle born into the new `video-edit` category** (the other 4 were reclassified into it). Same LTX 2.3 IC-LoRA depth-guided vid2vid pipeline: a **source video** (`199 LoadVideo`, a rough 3D render) is sliced to ~5s and depth-mapped (MoGe `697:*` branch), a **reference image** (`200 LoadImage`) sets the look/first-frame, and the **`3DREAL-strong` LoRA** (`129:195 LoraLoaderModelOnly`, used as the IC-LoRA via `129:196 GetICLoRAParameters`) restyles the clip into a **photoreal** result with a matching audio track (`129:122 CreateVideo` → `68 SaveVideo`). Distilled 8-step KSampler (`129:704`, cfg 1). Exposes **reference image / source video / Enhance-prompt-with-AI toggle / two gated prompt boxes / width / height / clip length / seed** — mirrors the sibling's 9 params. **Two edits vs. the candidate export:** (1) pruned the `129:210 PreviewAny` prompt-preview OUTPUT_NODE (would dump the prompt text into the job gallery — standard preview-sink cleanup; 45→44 nodes); (2) **fixed the `disabledWhen` gating the sibling ships broken** — the sibling points its two prompt boxes' `disabledWhen.param` at `enhance_prompt_129_212`, which **isn't an exposed key** (the toggle's real key is `primitiveboolean_value_129_212`), so its grey-out is silently inert (`disabled = values[dw.param] === dw.equals` never matches an undefined lookup); the new bundle references the real key, so the inactive prompt box actually greys out. **★ Fix the sibling too if it's ever touched.** Template copied **verbatim** (openable in ComfyUI), canonical `<id>` name. Models: `ltx-2.3-22b-distilled-fp8` + `gemma_3_12B_it_fp4_mixed` CLIP + **`3DREAL-strong.safetensors`** (327 MB; a `-light` variant also sits on the rig) + `gemma-3-12b-it-abliterated_lora_rank64` + `moge_2_vitl_normal_fp16` — **all 5 confirmed on the rig** (4 shared with the verified sibling; only the 3DREAL LoRA is new). `category` **video-edit**; `minVRAM 16`. Calibration cache-immune (`129:704.seed` literal → `_randomizeSeeds`; video auto-resolves smallest asset, image a median). The 3 unreachable-from-`SaveVideo` nodes (`697:23/45/696`, dead MoGe depth-preview leftovers of a muted export preview) are inert regular nodes the verified sibling also carries — not OUTPUT_NODEs, never executed. Validated off-rig (registry load + `hasTemplate`, all 9 params → real node/field & settable-literal, both `disabledWhen` keys resolve, one SaveVideo & no Preview* sinks, seed literal, candidate hidden). Pending rig verification.

**Taxonomy split — generation vs. editing, new `video-edit` group (2026-07-06)** — the workflow taxonomy now separates **making new media from modifying media the user supplies**. New **`video-edit` category** added to the [schemas.js](server/config/schemas.js) enum (alongside the pre-existing `image-edit`), and the admin-library filter chips ([WorkflowSelector.jsx](client/src/components/WorkflowSelector.jsx)) grew from one *Image generation* / *Video generation* pair into **four** groups — **Image generation** (`t2i`, wand) · **Image editing** (`image-edit`, brush) · **Video generation** (`i2v`, video) · **Video editing** (`video-edit`, film) — via a reworked `CATEGORY_GROUP` (`image-edit`/`video-edit` each get their own group; `t2i`→image, `i2v`→video). **`i2v` stays generation** — a video grown from an image is *not* an edit (owner's explicit call) — as do `t2v`/`flf2v`/`ia2v`/LivePortrait `image2video`. **Reclassified `i2v`→`video-edit`** (4 genuine video→video edits): the 3 Bernini editors `video_edit_bernini_r_video_editing{,_with_reference,_ref_autoprompt}` + the LTX depth restyle `video_edit_ltx2_3_ic_lora_vid2vid`. The 10 existing `image-edit` bundles auto-move into the new **Image editing** group; upscalers/segmentation/interpolation stay in **Utilities** (`i2i`/`preprocessor`). Mirrored across **all five taxonomy touchpoints**: the zod enum, the `GET /workflows` category-label map ([workflows.js](server/routes/workflows.js), "Video Editing"), the admin editor's category dropdown ([WorkflowMetaEditor.jsx](client/src/components/admin/WorkflowMetaEditor.jsx)), the library chips, and the **ComfyQ Discovery** desktop app ([renderer.js](desktop/renderer/renderer.js) — added `brush`/`film` inline-SVG icons + `GROUP_ICON`/`GROUP_LABEL` entries; the `groupFromId` stale-peer fallback now routes `vid2vid`/`*_video_editing`/`inpaint`/`*_edit`/`*_angles` ids to the edit groups). Registry validates all metas (zod accepts `video-edit`); post-split distribution = t2i 4 / image-edit 10 / i2v 5 / video-edit 4 / audio 1 / 3d 1 / description 2 / preprocessor 3 / i2i 1. Client build green. **★ Convention:** a new *editing* workflow (modifies a supplied image/video) uses `image-edit`/`video-edit`; a *generation* workflow (t2i/i2v/t2v/flf2v/animate-a-still) does not. **Supersedes the generation/edit lumping described in the 2026-06-21 "filter by type" entry below.**

**Qwen Image-Edit — scene camera angle (2026-07-06)** — [image_edit_qwen_multiple_scene_angles](workflows/image_edit_qwen_multiple_scene_angles/), the **scene sibling** of [image_edit_qwen_multiple_character_angles](workflows/image_edit_qwen_multiple_character_angles/) — same one-view-per-run image→image re-camera (upload a scene, describe the camera move, schedule each angle or invent your own; the same vetted set is seeded in the description). The owner pre-collapsed the candidate to a single angle; promoted **near-verbatim** — the only edit is pruning the one redundant `65:33:32 PreviewImage` sink (it duplicated the `31 SaveImage` output → a second identical gallery image), the standard preview-sink cleanup. 17→16 nodes, one output, template shipped, canonical name. **Differs from the character sibling in models — the 2509 line:** UNET `qwen_image_edit_2509_fp8_e4m3fn` (20 GB fp8, lighter than the character's 39 GB 2511 bf16) + `Qwen-Edit-2509-Multiple-angles` LoRA + `Qwen-Image-Edit-2509-Lightning-4steps` LoRA + the shared `qwen_2.5_vl_7b` CLIP + `qwen_image_vae` — all confirmed on the rig. The camera-angle field is a dedicated `66 PrimitiveStringMultiline` ("Prompt for new view") → the positive `65:33:17 TextEncodeQwenImageEditPlus` (vs. the character's prompt-on-the-encode-node). Exposes **scene image / camera-angle prompt / seed**. `category` **image-edit**; `minVRAM 20`. Calibration cache-immune (`65:33:21.seed` literal → `_randomizeSeeds`); image auto-resolves from `assets.dir`. Validated off-rig (schema, 3 params → node/field, 16 nodes reachable from the 1 output, no preview sinks, `66.value`→positive-encode wired, registry load + `hasTemplate`). Pending rig verification. *(Both Qwen multi-angle candidates are now promoted; the `image_qwen_multiple_angles` staging folder can be retired.)*

**Admin card "Stop serving" button (2026-07-06)** — the per-card serving control in [WorkflowSelector.jsx](client/src/components/WorkflowSelector.jsx) gains a **Stop serving** button beside the green "Serving" badge on the active card. It calls the existing `resetToAdmin` ([AdminConfig.jsx](client/src/pages/AdminConfig.jsx) → `POST /admin/reset-to-admin`) — flip the server back to **admin mode** (students can no longer book) + restart — the same graceful stop as the header "Reset to admin" button, now reachable right on the card being served (which matters because `/admin` stays accessible mid-serve, so the admin opens it while serving and stops from the card). **The "Serving" state is now mode-gated:** new `serving` prop (`config.mode === 'student'`) → `isServing = isActive && serving`, so a card shows "Serving" + the stop button only when the server is *actually* serving it in student mode; in admin mode the last-active card shows "Activate & serve" instead of a misleading green badge. The delete-disable guard stays on `isActive` (still can't delete the configured-active workflow, even in admin mode, to avoid a dangling `activeWorkflowId`). No new server route (reuses `reset-to-admin`); the `deactivating` state drives the button spinner. Client build green.

**Qwen Image-Edit — character camera angle (2026-07-06)** — [image_edit_qwen_multiple_character_angles](workflows/image_edit_qwen_multiple_character_angles/), a **verbatim** `_candidate_workflows/` promotion (the character sibling of the still-staged `Qwen_multiple_scene_angles`; the old `image_qwen_multiple_angles` was the scene version, moved to staging in the tree cleanup). **Image→image re-camera, one view per run:** upload a subject (`25 LoadImage`), describe the camera move in the **Camera angle** field (`427:221:151 TextEncodeQwenImageEditPlus.prompt`), and Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 (+ the multiple-angles LoRA + the 4-step Lightning LoRA, fast path on) re-renders the same subject from that viewpoint → `373 SaveImage`. To build a full turnaround, **schedule it once per angle** (or invent new ones); the description seeds the vetted set to paste — close-up / wide-angle lens / aerial view / low-angle view / 45° & 90° left+right / 180° back view / profile side view (the model's `"Turn/Rotate the camera to…"` grammar). Exposes **image / camera-angle prompt / seed**. **Verbatim api.json (25 nodes, one `SaveImage`, all reachable) + `<id>_template.json`** — the owner pre-collapsed the candidate to a single `427:221:*` subgraph (the earlier 8-parallel-angle export is retired), so ComfyQ promotes it **unchanged**: no graph surgery, the template ships (openable in ComfyUI), canonical `<id>` name kept. Models: `qwen_image_edit_2511_bf16` (a **39 GB bf16** UNET — bigger than the 5090's 32 GB → ComfyUI weight-streams it) + `qwen_2.5_vl_7b` CLIP + `qwen_image_vae` + the two LoRAs — all confirmed on the rig. `category` **image-edit**; `minVRAM 24`. Calibration cache-immune (`427:221:169.seed` literal → `_randomizeSeeds`); image auto-resolves from `assets.dir`. The earlier 8-angle version had been gauged on the rig (49 s warm / 167 s cold incl. 118 s model load for the 39 GB model), so the models/node-stack are confirmed working; the single-angle path still needs its own gauge. Validated off-rig (schema, 3 params → node/field, 25/25 reachable from the 1 output, seed literal, registry load + `hasTemplate`). **★ Pattern note (learned the hard way here):** promote candidates **verbatim** — copy the api + `<id>_template.json` under the canonical `<id>` name; don't restructure the graph, rename the bundle, or drop the template. If the deployed graph genuinely must differ, the owner reshapes the *candidate* first (as here). Pending rig verification (single-angle). *(The `Qwen_multiple_scene_angles` scene candidate is still staged.)*

**Krea-2 Turbo t2i + a dynamic, filtered LoRA dropdown (2026-07-06)** — [image_krea2_turbo_t2i_lora](workflows/image_krea2_turbo_t2i_lora/), the **6th `_candidate_workflows/` promotion** (source `Krea2_turbo_t2i_lora_{api,template}.json`), and the **first server-populated dropdown** param. Krea-2 Turbo is an 8-step distilled t2i (`krea2_turbo_fp8_scaled` + `qwen3vl_4b` CLIP + `qwen_image_vae`); the graph adds a **Qwen LLM prompt-enhancer** (`30:16 TextGenerate`, gated by `30:24` "Refine prompt?") and an **Enable-LoRA switch** (`30:23`) that swaps the base model for a `LoraLoaderModelOnly` (`30:15`) via `30:22 ComfySwitchNode`. Exposes **prompt / aspect ratio / apply-style-LoRA / Style LoRA (dropdown) / LoRA strength / seed** (the prompt-enhancer `30:24` stays on via its literal — the owner dropped its toggle from the exposed set in the editor). **New `lora` param type — a dynamic, prefix-filtered dropdown:** the user wanted a LoRA picker limited to Krea-compatible files (`krea2_*` in `models/loras`), realism default. Unlike `select` (static options in the meta), a `lora` param's options are filled **at booking time** by scanning ComfyUI's model dir. Infra: **`ParamType` += `lora`** ([schemas.js](server/config/schemas.js)) + optional `optionsDir` (subdir under `models/`, default `loras`) + `optionsFilter` (case-insensitive filename prefix) on `ExposedParameter`; **[modelOptions.js](server/workflows/modelOptions.js)** `listModelFiles(comfyRoot, subdir, prefix)` scans `<root>/models/<subdir>` on the ComfyQ **host disk** (NOT via ComfyUI's API, so it works whether ComfyUI is up or not), filters by ext+prefix, sorts, **8 s TTL cache** (the realtime bus rebuilds the parameter_map on every broadcast — many/min); missing dir / unset root → `[]`; **[realtimeBus.js](server/realtime/realtimeBus.js)** `_buildParameterMap(exposed, comfyRoot)` sets `options` + prettified `optionLabels` (ext stripped) for `lora` params, always keeping the `default` selectable so it never renders empty; **[DynamicParamFields.jsx](client/src/components/DynamicParamFields.jsx)** renders `lora` via the shared `select` branch (+`optionLabels`, disabled "No compatible LoRAs found" when empty), so the WorkflowMetaEditor "Student preview" gets it too — it fetches the same list from a new **`GET /admin/loras?dir=&filter=`** ([admin.js](server/routes/admin.js)). The wire value is just the `.safetensors` filename → the **generic materializer** sets `30:15.lora_name` with **zero exec changes** (rides the `mask`/`select` path). Round-trip: `optionsDir`/`optionsFilter` added to the editor save payload + edit-data merge (the `disabledWhen` pattern) so a re-save keeps the filter (the base meta.json owns the type; config-overrides only patch label/default/order/enabled). **Bundle edits (not verbatim):** bypassed + deleted the in-path `30:20 PreviewAny` (rewired its two consumers to its source `30:21`) — an OUTPUT_NODE that would dump the AI-expanded prompt as a stray text result; neutralized the hardcoded wrong trigger word (`30:27.string_b`/delimiter → `""`, since the flattened graph lost the per-LoRA CustomCombo mapping — users add a trigger in the prompt); flipped `30:23` (Enable LoRA) to **true** so the realism LoRA is applied by default. 22→21 nodes, all reachable from `29 SaveImage`; **all comfy-core** (no custom-node deps). `category` **t2i**; `minVRAM 16`. Calibration cache-immune (`30:3.seed` literal → `_randomizeSeeds`), pure t2i (no media inputs); the default path (LoRA-on + refine-on) is timed. Validated off-rig (schema accepts `lora`+filter, all 6 params resolve, graph edits landed, `listModelFiles` filters/sorts/labels + missing-dir fallback, realtimeBus populates the dropdown, candidate hidden, client build green). **Aspect ratio (2026-07-06 follow-up):** now exposed as a plain `select` on the `49 ResolutionSelector` node (`aspect_ratio`), default `1:1 (Square)`. The 8 option strings were read **live** from the rig's `GET /object_info/ResolutionSelector` (ComfyUI was up) and the meta's list is verified to match the enum **exactly** — so a non-default pick can't hit ComfyUI's "value not in list" hard-fail (the reason best-guessing these was avoided). `ResolutionSelector` computes `width`/`height` from `aspect_ratio` × `megapixels`(1) × `multiple`(8); output size follows the picked ratio. Meta-only change (standard select, existing render) — no restart, registry mtime-invalidates. **★ Rig-watch:** (1) `comfy_ui.root_path` must point at the install for the LoRA scan (else the dropdown shows only the realism default); (2) confirm the `krea2_*` LoRAs list + realism default loads; (3) confirm the LLM enhancer's timing is acceptable as the default. Pending rig verification.

**Flux.2 Klein reference + depth control — pose transfer (2026-07-06)** — [image_edit_flux2_klein_ref_control_depth](workflows/image_edit_flux2_klein_ref_control_depth/), the **5th `_candidate_workflows/` promotion** (source: `Flux2_klein_ref_control_depth_{api,template}.json`). A **two-image reference-control** generator: a **Structure image** (`119 LoadImage` "Pose") is turned into a depth map (`145 ImageScaleToTotalPixels` 1 MP → `129 DepthAnything_V3` `da3_large`, auto-downloaded) that drives the composition, while a **Reference image** (`81 LoadImage`) supplies the appearance; both are VAE-encoded and fed as **`ReferenceLatent`** chains (depth latent `140` + reference latent `133`, on **both** the positive `113→141→134` and negative `114→139→132` conditioning) into a 4-step Klein `SamplerCustomAdvanced` (`CFGGuider` cfg 1, `Flux2Scheduler` steps 4) running the **`flux2_klein_9b_refcontrol_depth` LoRA** (`117 LoraLoaderModelOnly`) → `102 VAEDecode` → `146 SaveImage`. So the output takes the **structure/pose of one image and the subject/style of another** — pose a character, restage a product, re-render a scene's layout with new content. **Output dims follow the Structure image** — `106 GetImageSize` (off the depth image) feeds **both** `116 Flux2Scheduler` and `107 EmptyFlux2LatentImage`, already a shared source, so **no width/height graph edit was needed** (unlike `image_flux2_dev_t2i`). Exposes **reference image / structure image / prompt / negative prompt / seed**; steps + cfg + megapixels stay fixed. **Zero server code**; the only edit was **pruning two `PreviewImage` OUTPUT_NODE sinks** — `130` (a duplicate of the saved final image) and `131` (the intermediate depth map) — so neither clutters the result gallery (31→29 nodes, all reachable from `146 SaveImage`). `category` **image-edit** → Image generation group; `minVRAM 18`. Calibration is **cache-immune** — the literal seed lives on the **rgthree `143 Seed`** node (`143.seed`, bumped by `_randomizeSeeds`) and propagates to `103 RandomNoise.noise_seed` via a link; both image inputs auto-resolve from `assets.dir` and DepthAnything works on any picture (no face needed, unlike LivePortrait), so the admin gauge calibrates cleanly.
