Validated off-rig (schema, all 5 params → node/field incl. the rgthree seed, 29/29 reachable, dims-in-sync, registry load + `hasTemplate`, candidate hidden; parser detects the 5 exposed widgets through the custom `DepthAnything_V3`/`Seed (rgthree)` nodes via primitive-fallback). **★ Rig-watch — new dependencies:** confirm the **`ComfyUI-DepthAnythingV3`** custom node (+ `da3_large.safetensors`) and the **`flux2_klein_9b_refcontrol_depth.safetensors`** LoRA are present on the rig (the other 3 models — klein-9b / qwen3-8b / flux2-vae — already ship with the inpaint siblings); and confirm whether the prompt's default **`"refcontrol"`** is a required trigger word or just describable text (the node is titled "+ Describe Or Not Reference Image", so it's exposed **optional** with that safe default). Pending rig verification.

**Mask painter — Invert button (2026-07-06)** — [MaskDrawField.jsx](client/src/components/capture/MaskDrawField.jsx) gains an **Invert** button (toolbar, next to Undo/Clear) that swaps the painted ↔ unpainted regions — the common "paint the subject to keep, then flip to replace the background" move. Builds the inverse alpha by filling a temp canvas with the brush colour and knocking the current mask out of it (`destination-out`), then `redraw()` + `commit()` so the exported RGBA PNG (still `1−alpha` → MASK) reflects the flip immediately; snapshots for undo first (one Undo reverts it) and counts as a stroke (keeps Undo/Clear enabled), disabled at `strokes === 0`. Client build green.

**Mask painter moved to a near-fullscreen modal (2026-07-05)** — [MaskDrawField.jsx](client/src/components/capture/MaskDrawField.jsx) (the `mask` param widget) painted on a canvas capped at `48vh` **inline inside the booking form** — far too cramped to mask precisely. Now: uploading an image drops the user straight into a **near-fullscreen paint modal** where the canvas fills the viewport (`flex-1 min-h-0` container + `max-w/max-h:100%, w/h:auto` → biggest possible while preserving aspect); the booking form shows a small **composited preview** (base + translucent mask overlay via a `toDataURL` `<img>`) with a **"Paint / Edit mask"** button to reopen. Added a **live brush-outline cursor** (a circle at the pointer, radius = brush/2, drawn each `redraw()` from a `brushRef` + `hoverPt` ref) so strokes land precisely, a brush-size readout, and a wider size range (4–200 px). **The modal is `createPortal`-ed to `document.body`** — essential because it renders *inside* the BookingDialog modal, and the shared [Modal](client/src/components/ui/Modal.jsx) applies a `zoom-in-95` transform, which would make a nested `position:fixed` overlay relative to the dialog box instead of the viewport (portal escapes that stacking context; `z-[100]` sits above the dialog's `z-50`). Esc closes it; body scroll is locked while open (restoring the *prior* overflow, since BookingDialog's Modal already set it). **Export is byte-identical** — `commit()` (the `destination-out` base+mask composite → RGBA PNG), `strokeTo`, and the offscreen `maskRef` geometry are all unchanged, and the `onChange(file)`/`onRemove` props are the same, so the pipeline (`/upload` → materializer → `LoadImage`) and every consumer are untouched; only the painting *surface* changed. Also now emits the file on upload (empty mask → base unchanged) so the field satisfies like a normal image upload, and the shared [DynamicParamFields](client/src/components/DynamicParamFields.jsx) means the WorkflowMetaEditor "Student preview" gets the same modal for free. Client build green.

**Flux.2 Klein reference-guided inpainting bundle (2026-07-05)** — [image_edit_flux2_klein_inpaint_reference](workflows/image_edit_flux2_klein_inpaint_reference/), the **reference-image sibling** of [image_edit_flux2_klein_inpaint_prompt](workflows/image_edit_flux2_klein_inpaint_prompt/) (4th `_candidate_workflows/` promotion; source `flux2_klein_inpaint_reference_image_{api,template}.json`, renamed to the canonical `<id>.*`). Same paint-a-mask Klein inpaint pipeline **plus a second image input** — the **reference** (`22 LoadImage` → `23 ImageScaleToTotalPixels` 1 MP → `24 VAEEncode`), whose latent is chained via two extra `ReferenceLatent` nodes (`25` positive / `26` negative, after `InpaintModelConditioning`) onto the conditioning, so Klein brings the reference's subject/style **into** the painted area ("put this product on that shelf"). **Two media inputs:** node `1` = scene → **`mask`** type (paint where it goes), node `22` = reference → **`image`** type (upload the what); both auto-resolve for calibration (mask stages a plain image, reference resolves a median asset). Exposes **scene (paint-mask) / reference image / positive prompt / negative prompt / seed**; steps (4) + cfg (1) + crop + reference-megapixels stay fixed. **Zero server code, verbatim api.json** (25 nodes, all reachable from `20 SaveImage`, no preview/dead nodes → no pruning). Models all present on the rig (`flux-2-klein-9b-fp8` / `qwen_3_8b_fp8mixed` / `flux2-vae`); `comfyui-inpaint-cropandstitch` installed; `category` **image-edit**; `minVRAM 18`; cache-immune calibration (`15 RandomNoise.noise_seed` literal). Validated off-rig (schema, both media params + 3 text/number resolve, 25/25 reachable, registry load + `hasTemplate`, candidate hidden). Same **★ rig-watch** as the prompt sibling: mask polarity (only the painted region changes) + empty-mask calibration. With this, **both** candidate inpaint siblings are promoted. Pending rig verification.

**Flux.2 Klein prompt-guided inpainting bundle — re-added (2026-07-05)** — [image_edit_flux2_klein_inpaint_prompt](workflows/image_edit_flux2_klein_inpaint_prompt/), the third `_candidate_workflows/` promotion (source: `flux2_klein_inpaint_prompt_{api,template}.json`). **Re-adds** the paint-a-mask inpainting that the removed `image_flux2_inpaint` bundle used to cover (it was deleted in the workflow-tree cleanup — no inpaint bundle existed until now), rebuilt on the **`ComfyUI-Inpaint-CropAndStitch`** node pack (`comfyui-inpaint-cropandstitch`, confirmed installed on the rig): `1 LoadImage` → `2 InpaintCropImproved` (cuts a 1024-px context window around the mask so Klein works at native res) → 4-step distilled Klein sampler (`CFGGuider` cfg 1, `Flux2Scheduler` steps 4) → `19 InpaintStitchImproved` (blends back into the full image) → `20 SaveImage`. The painted region is regenerated from the **positive prompt**; `ReferenceLatent` feeds the full cropped-source latent as edit context so the new content blends with the scene. **Uses the existing `mask` param type** (added 2026-06-25, infra survived the cleanup — schema enum, [MaskDrawField.jsx](client/src/components/capture/MaskDrawField.jsx), [DynamicParamFields.jsx](client/src/components/DynamicParamFields.jsx)): node **`1 LoadImage`** provides both the base (IMAGE out 0) and the mask (MASK out 1 = `1−alpha`) from **one painted RGBA PNG** — the in-browser painter makes the area-to-replace transparent. Exposes **paint-a-mask image / positive prompt / negative prompt / seed**; steps (4) + cfg (1) + the crop settings stay fixed. **Zero server code, verbatim api.json** — the export was already clean (20 nodes, all reachable from `SaveImage`, no `PreviewImage`/comparer/dead nodes → no pruning, unlike the old removed bundle which needed an `Image Comparer` prune). Models all present on the rig (`flux-2-klein-9b-fp8` / `qwen_3_8b_fp8mixed` / `flux2-vae`); `category` **image-edit** → Image generation group; `minVRAM 18`. Calibration cache-immune (`15 RandomNoise.noise_seed` literal → `_randomizeSeeds`); the `mask` input auto-resolves to a plain staged image (no alpha → empty mask → no-op inpaint, but the 4-step sampler still runs → valid timing). Validated off-rig (schema, param→node/field resolution incl. the `mask` type, 20/20 reachable, registry load + `hasTemplate`, candidate hidden). The `image_edit_flux2_klein_inpaint_reference` sibling (adds a reference image) is still in `_candidate_workflows/`. **★ Rig-watch — mask polarity:** confirm only the *painted* region regenerates (standard ComfyUI white=inpaint; `InpaintCropImproved.mask_invert:false` agrees) — if inverted, flip in `MaskDrawField.commit()`; and confirm calibration survives the empty-mask stage (if `InpaintCropImproved` chokes on a zero mask, calibrate via a real booked run). Pending rig verification.

**Flux.2 Dev text-to-image bundle (2026-07-05)** ⚠ **REMOVED 2026-10-05 (owner) with its 85.76 GB of weights — see §6d of the redundancy doc.** — [image_flux2_dev_t2i](workflows/image_flux2_dev_t2i/), the **t2i sibling** of [image_edit_flux2_dev_image_edit](workflows/image_edit_flux2_dev_image_edit/) and the second `_candidate_workflows/` promotion (source: `Flux2_dev_t2i_{api,template}.json`). Pure **text → image** Flux.2 Dev: prompt (`98:6 CLIPTextEncode` → `98:26 FluxGuidance` 4) → `SamplerCustomAdvanced` (`98:13`) over an `EmptyFlux2LatentImage` (`98:47`) → `VAEDecode` → `9 SaveImage`; single CLIPTextEncode (embedded guidance, no negative/CFG). Same **"Fast mode"** `PrimitiveBoolean` (`98:104`) driving two `ComfySwitchNode`s (model base↔Turbo-LoRA, steps 20↔8). Exposes **prompt / width / height / Fast mode / seed**. **★ One graph edit (not verbatim) — shared width/height:** the flattened export left `width`/`height` as **separate literal `1024`s on two nodes** (`98:47` EmptyLatent + `98:48` Flux2Scheduler), so one exposed param couldn't keep them in sync (and the scheduler's resolution must match the latent's). Fixed by adding two `PrimitiveInt` source nodes (`98:200` Width / `98:201` Height) and rewiring **both** consumers to them, so one param drives both — mirroring the **verified `image_flux2_klein_9b_t2i`** (`75:68`/`75:69` → `75:66`+`75:62`). Built programmatically from the candidate export (add nodes + rewire), not hand-edited. **CLIP note:** the api.json uses `mistral_3_small_flux2_**fp8**.safetensors` (the edit sibling used `**bf16**`) — **both confirmed present on the rig** (`models/text_encoders/`), along with the other 3 (`flux2_dev_fp8mixed`, `full_encoder_small_decoder`, `Flux_2-Turbo-LoRA_comfyui`), so it's available, not broken. `category` **t2i** → Image generation group; `minVRAM 24`. Calibration cache-immune (`98:25 RandomNoise.noise_seed` literal → `_randomizeSeeds`); `warmupParams` sets a short prompt and **leaves Fast mode off** so the estimate reflects the default 20-step path. Validated off-rig (schema, param→node/field resolution, 21/21 reachable from `SaveImage`, width+height both point at the same source, registry load + `hasTemplate`, candidate folder hidden). Pending rig verification.

**Admin "ComfyUI backend" card — Restart button + the LAN toggle moved here (2026-07-05)** — the *ComfyUI backend* card in [AdminConfig.jsx](client/src/pages/AdminConfig.jsx) gains a **Restart ComfyUI** button and now hosts the **"Expose ComfyUI to the LAN"** toggle (moved out of the ComfyUI Settings/paths card, where it was a `pathDraft` field only persisted by *Save settings*). The toggle now **saves immediately on its own** (`PUT /admin/comfy {lan_access}`, mirroring the federation "Network presence" toggle) and reads from `config.comfy_ui.lan_access`. **Restart** = new route `POST /admin/comfyui/restart` → new **`AdminCalibrator.restartBackend()`** ([adminCalibrator.js](server/benchmark/adminCalibrator.js)): it stops the ComfyUI **we own** and relaunches it reading config **fresh**, so an edited path *or* a flipped LAN toggle takes effect; the bind **follows the saved `lan_access`** (`network: !!comfyConfig.lan_access`) so the toggle is the single source of truth. **External-ComfyUI "take over" (2026-07-05 b, owner-chosen):** when the running ComfyUI is **external** (started outside ComfyQ — the common workshop setup where the admin runs the portable `.bat` and ComfyQ merely attaches), Restart doesn't refuse; it **force-kills the process on the port and relaunches ComfyQ's own**, so ComfyQ manages it afterward (Restart/Stop/LAN-toggle/opener all then work). New helper **`killProcessOnPort(port)`** in [comfyProcess.js](server/workers/comfyProcess.js) (Windows `netstat -ano` → `taskkill /F /T`; POSIX `lsof` → SIGKILL; best-effort, verified regex against real `netstat` output incl. IPv4+IPv6 dedupe), then `_waitForPortFree()` polls `/system_stats` until dead (so the respawn doesn't re-detect the dying instance as external), then `_ensureWorker` spawns fresh (`tookOver:true`). It's **admin-initiated only** — the client gates it behind a **confirm modal** (`showTakeoverConfirm`) since it closes the admin's manually-started ComfyUI; the button appears in the previously button-less `external` branch as **"Restart & take over"**. To keep the two coherent, **"Launch/Bind on network" now also persists `lan_access:true`** (`launchComfy` PUTs `{...pathDraft, lan_access:true}`), so the toggle reflects reality and a later Restart stays on the network instead of silently dropping to localhost (recall `bindHost = lan_access ? '0.0.0.0' : host`, and `_workerNetwork` — hence the `networkBound` badge — must match the actual bind, which is why restart routes `lan_access` through the `network` flag). Restart + Stop render only for a **running, ComfyQ-owned** backend (non-external, non-student); the toggle is always visible (persists in student mode too, applying on the next activation). **Needs a server restart to load the new route.** Client build green; pending rig check.

**Flux.2 Dev image editing bundle — verified on rig (2026-07-05)** ⚠ **REMOVED 2026-10-05 (owner) with its 85.76 GB of weights.** — [image_edit_flux2_dev_image_edit](workflows/image_edit_flux2_dev_image_edit/), the first **candidate-folder promotion** since the `_candidate_workflows/` staging area was introduced (source: `workflows/_candidate_workflows/Flux2_dev_image_edit_{api,template}.json`). **Kontext-style image edit:** the source image (`46 LoadImage`) is scaled to 1 MP (`45 ImageScaleToTotalPixels`), VAE-encoded (`68:44`), and fed as a **`ReferenceLatent`** (`68:43`) alongside the text prompt (`68:6 CLIPTextEncode` → `68:26 FluxGuidance` guidance 4) into a `SamplerCustomAdvanced` (`68:13`) — so the model edits the picture instead of generating from scratch; output dims follow the input aspect via `68:72 GetImageSize`. Flux.2 Dev uses embedded guidance (no negative/CFG → **single** CLIPTextEncode, unlike the Klein edit sibling). A **"Fast mode" checkbox** (`68:94 PrimitiveBoolean` "Enable 8 steps lora") drives **two** `ComfySwitchNode`s at once — model (`68:92`: base vs `68:89` Turbo-LoRA) and steps (`68:93`: `68:91`=20 vs `68:90`=8) — so on = 8-step Turbo-LoRA (fast), off = 20-step base (quality). Exposes **source image / edit instruction / Fast mode / seed** (order 0–3); steps/guidance/megapixels stay fixed. **Zero server code, verbatim api.json** — the export was already clean (24 nodes, all reachable from `9 SaveImage`, no `PreviewAny`/dead nodes), so no pruning. `category` **image-edit** → Image generation group; 4 models (UNET `flux2_dev_fp8mixed`, VAE `full_encoder_small_decoder`, CLIP `mistral_3_small_flux2_bf16`, LoRA `Flux_2-Turbo-LoRA_comfyui`); `minVRAM 24` (best-guess — Flux.2 Dev fp8mixed is heavy). Calibration is **cache-immune** (`68:25 RandomNoise.noise_seed` is a literal seed → `_randomizeSeeds` bumps it) with the image auto-resolved from `assets.dir`; `warmupParams` sets only a short prompt (leaves Fast mode **off** so the measured `estimatedDurationSec` reflects the default 20-step path). Named `image_edit_flux2_dev_image_edit` to match the `image_edit_flux2_klein_9b_image_edit` sibling pattern. **✅ Verified on rig (2026-07-05):** edits run end-to-end; the admin gauge calibrated the **default 20-step path** — `runtime.json` shows `steps:20` (confirming warmup correctly left Fast mode **off**, so the estimate reflects the default), **warm ≈45 s** / cold ≈203 s incl. **≈158 s model load** on an RTX 5090 (Flux.2 Dev fp8mixed is a heavy cold load but runs fine on the 32 GB card, so `minVRAM 24` holds as a floor). Off-rig validation (schema, param→node/field resolution, full reachability from `SaveImage`, registry load + `hasTemplate`, candidate folder stays hidden) also green. The bundle ships the calibration `runtime.json` (tracked, like the sibling bundles).

**"Open in ComfyUI" now truly auto-opens the workflow (opener extension + one-time restart) (2026-07-01)** — replaces the "stage → pick it from the Workflows sidebar" handoff with a real auto-open. **Why the old way had to change / what's technically possible:** ComfyUI **1.45.20** has no URL param to open an arbitrary workflow, and its startup tab-restore state is **browser localStorage keyed by clientId** (`Comfy.Workflow.OpenPaths:<clientId>` — verified in the frontend bundle), so it's **not server-writable** and a process restart alone can't drive it; we also can't script the cross-origin ComfyUI tab. The only reliable lever is a **tiny ComfyUI frontend extension** we ship: [server/comfyui/opener/](server/comfyui/opener/) (`__init__.py` with `WEB_DIRECTORY` + `js/comfyq_opener.js`) registers `ComfyQ.Opener`, reads a **custom `?comfyq_open=<id>` URL param**, fetches the staged workflow via the same-origin userdata API, and `app.loadGraphData(graph, true, true)` lands it on the canvas (it waits for the app graph + a grace period because ComfyUI's own restore runs *after* extension `setup()` and would otherwise overwrite it; then strips the param so a refresh doesn't clobber edits). The server **auto-installs** it into `<comfy root>/custom_nodes/comfyq_opener` ([server/comfyui/openerExtension.js](server/comfyui/openerExtension.js), version-marked so edits refresh) — [adminCalibrator.js](server/benchmark/adminCalibrator.js) installs it **before every ComfyUI spawn** and tracks `_openerLoaded`; new `ensureOpenerLoaded()` launches ComfyUI if down (opener present at boot → loaded, no restart) or, if a ComfyUI we own is already running without it, **restarts it once** to load it (the accepted "restart" tradeoff — a **one-time** cost, then restart-free). New route **`POST /admin/workflows/:id/open-in-comfyui`** ([admin.js](server/routes/admin.js)) stages the template + calls `ensureOpenerLoaded()` and returns `{port, autoOpen, openName}`; [useComfyOpener.js](client/src/hooks/useComfyOpener.js) opens `<comfyui>/?comfyq_open=<openName>` when `autoOpen`, else falls back to the **Workflows-sidebar** handoff (external ComfyUI we can't restart) or **download+drag** (409, no template). The old `stage-template` route remains as a harmless subset. **★ Two fixes after the first cut (2026-07-01 b):** (1) **staged name is `<id>_template.json`, not `<id>.json`** — staging as `<id>.json` overwrote an admin's own hand-saved ComfyUI workflow of the same id; the `_template` name avoids that and matches the bundle template's name. (2) **`loadGraphData` must match ComfyUI's own template loader** — `app.loadGraphData(graph, true, true, name, {openSource:'template'})`; the original call omitted the 4th (`workflowName`) + 5th (`openSource`) args, which are what register **subgraph definitions** — without them a subgraph workflow (most of ours) loads **broken/unrunnable**. Bump `OPENER_VERSION` in [openerExtension.js](server/comfyui/openerExtension.js) when editing the extension so it re-installs + reloads. **★ Third fix — canvas-timing crash (2026-07-03, opener v3):** the loader then aborted with **`getCanvas: canvas is null`** (inside `loadGraphData`→`fitView`). Root cause: extension `setup()` runs **before ComfyUI's Vue `<GraphCanvas>` finishes mounting**, so `app.canvas` is already truthy but the Vue **canvas store** `fitView()` reads is still null — worse on this rig because its large custom-node stack mounts the canvas late (the old fixed 500 ms grace wasn't enough). Fix in [comfyq_opener.js](server/comfyui/opener/js/comfyq_opener.js): (a) **`waitForCanvas()`** polls up to ~20 s for the real `<canvas>` element to be **DOM-connected + laid out** (`isConnected && clientWidth>0 && clientHeight>0`) — a reliable "mount complete → canvas store populated" signal — instead of just checking `app.canvas` truthiness; (b) the load runs **fire-and-forget after `setup()` returns** so we don't block the very mount we're waiting on; (c) **`loadWithRetry()`** retries `loadGraphData` 4× (500 ms apart) and, as a last resort, falls back to **`restore_view:false`** so the (already subgraph-registered) graph still lands even if the viewport-fit keeps racing; (d) all errors are **logged, never surfaced** as ComfyUI's "Loading aborted" dialog. **✅ Verified working on the rig (2026-07-03)** — a subgraph workflow auto-opens onto the canvas intact and runnable. **★ Operational gotcha — bump = browser hard-refresh (the thing that wasted a round here):** ComfyUI serves the extension JS from disk **per request**, so a running ComfyUI picks up a rewritten `comfyq_opener.js` with **no ComfyUI restart** — BUT the **browser HTTP-caches** the module at `/extensions/comfyq_opener/comfyq_opener.js`, so after an `OPENER_VERSION` bump the open tab keeps executing the *old* JS and the "fix" looks like it didn't take (diagnose by opening that URL directly, or grepping the on-disk file under `<comfy root>/custom_nodes/comfyq_opener/js/` for the new markers, and by noting the crash's `comfyq_opener.js:<line>` matches the old structure). **Fix: hard-refresh the ComfyUI tab once (Ctrl+Shift+R)** to evict the cached module; end users who load it once aren't affected — this only bites during our edit-iterate loop. **Rig-watch:** if a subgraph graph ever loads unframed or gets overwritten by ComfyUI's own restore, lengthen the post-canvas grace in `comfyq_opener.js`.

**Job ingredients — full relaunch info + portable "Download ingredients (.zip)" (2026-06-29)** — the completed-job panel ([client/src/components/ImageLightbox.jsx](client/src/components/ImageLightbox.jsx)) now shows **everything needed to relaunch a job**: a **Relaunch info** card with the **seed** (any `*seed` param, copy button), the **imported media** (param → original filename), and **every parameter** (media values prettified to their original upload name, not the `comfyq_*` filename). New **"Download ingredients (.zip)"** button (always available) bundles the imported media + a `settings.json` snapshot (workflow id, all params, seed, prompt) so a generation can be re-created later **even after the machine switched to a different workflow** — the cross-workflow problem the old in-app recall couldn't solve. **Why a zip, not in-app recall:** "Use these settings" recalls by looking up `initialParams[key]` against the *currently loaded* workflow's `parameter_map` ([BookingDialog.jsx](client/src/components/BookingDialog.jsx)), so keys silently mismatch when a different workflow is active; it's now **gated to show only when `job.workflow_id === state.workflow_info.id`** (the zip covers every other case). **Durability:** imported inputs are transient (the `comfyq__*` copies are swept ~30 min after upload by `InputUploader.sweepStale`), so on the **COMPLETED** transition the executor ([server/executor/jobExecutor.js](server/executor/jobExecutor.js)) snapshots the inputs + a settings.json into a **durable per-job store** ([server/storage/ingredientsStore.js](server/storage/ingredientsStore.js), `<comfy root>/comfyq_ingredients/<jobId>/{settings.json,README.txt,media/*}` — outside the input dir so the sweep can't touch it). The zip route `GET /jobs/:id/ingredients.zip` streams that folder via **`archiver`** (new server dep — rigs need `npm install` + restart); for pre-feature/old jobs it falls back to settings-only, generated live from the DB. **★ Where the media actually comes from:** `job.inputFiles` is **empty** in the normal v2 flow (the upload route stores each media param as a `comfyq_session__…` file in `ComfyUI/input` and the client puts that filename in `paramValues` — nothing populates `inputFiles` despite the [jobExecutor.js](server/executor/jobExecutor.js) comment), so `ingredientsStore.mediaRefs(job)` derives the imported media from the `comfyq_*` values in `paramValues` (merging `inputFiles` for the bench path); that same helper feeds the slim `input_files:[{param,name}]` on the wire job ([server/routes/jobs.js](server/routes/jobs.js) + [server/realtime/realtimeBus.js](server/realtime/realtimeBus.js)) so the UI's "Imported media" list is populated too. **Off-ComfyUI tested** (node test: persist copies the paramValues media with original names, extracts the seed, writes settings; wire `input_files` populated; zip route returns a valid PK zip with media for persisted jobs, 404s unknown, falls back to settings-only for non-persisted). Client + server build green.

**Desktop app — installers + GitHub auto-update + one-command release [shipped 2026-06-28; validated end-to-end on a real Windows machine: an installed v2.0.1 self-updated to v2.0.2 with zero interaction; three releases cut (v2.0.1/2/3)].** ComfyQ – Discovery (the `desktop/` Electron app) is now distributed as a **downloadable, self-updating installer**, not run-from-source-only. Stack: **electron-builder** (devDep) packages it; **electron-updater** (a real **dependency** — it's `require`d at runtime, so it MUST be a dep or electron-builder prunes it from the asar) pulls releases from the **public** `b2renger/ComfyQ` repo (public → no token on the client). **Release flow:** `cd desktop && npm run release:patch` (or `:minor`/`:major`) → bumps `desktop/package.json` → tags `vX.Y.Z` → pushes → [.github/workflows/release.yml](.github/workflows/release.yml) builds Win/Mac/Linux on a matrix and `electron-builder --publish always` uploads installers + `latest*.yml` manifests to a GitHub Release; installed apps pick it up on next launch. **★ npm-version gotcha (real, on this machine):** `npm version` **silently skips its git commit/tag step** here (git-tag-version is `true`, no `.npmrc` — npm likely can't reach `git` on its spawn PATH, the same reason the Bash tool can't), so `release:*` bumps with `--no-git-tag-version` and does the commit/tag/push **explicitly** via [desktop/scripts/release.mjs](desktop/scripts/release.mjs) (fails loudly, and only `git add`s the two version files so a dirty `config.json` is left alone — no stash needed). **electron-builder.yml:** added `publish:{provider:github,owner:b2renger,repo:ComfyQ}`, a mac **`zip`** target (REQUIRED for `latest-mac.yml` — Squirrel.Mac can't update from a `.dmg` alone), and **per-user one-click NSIS** (`oneClick:true,perMachine:false`) so silent updates need no UAC. **main.js:** electron-updater is **lazily `require`d inside an `app.isPackaged` guard** — a top-level `const {autoUpdater}=require('electron-updater')` **crashes at load** because the module getter constructs the updater (which calls `app.getVersion()`) eagerly, and it throws under `ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE=1` (which is set in this shell — it made `electron .` run as plain Node v24 in testing). Startup `checkForUpdates()` 3 s after window create; updater events forwarded to the renderer over one `update-status` channel; IPC `app:get-version` / `app:check-for-updates` / `app:quit-and-install`. **preload.js:** `getVersion`/`checkForUpdates`/`quitAndInstall`/`onUpdateStatus` added to `window.fleet`. **renderer:** an **"App version vX.Y.Z"** row + a **Check for updates** button in the ⚙ settings panel (`.controls`); flow is download-in-background → toast progress → button flips to **"Restart to update"** (single flag-driven handler, `quitAndInstall` via IPC), via the existing `showToast`. UX = `autoDownload:true` + `autoInstallOnAppQuit:true` (never interrupts a session). **`.gitignore`:** un-ignored `desktop/package-lock.json` (CI `npm ci` needs it committed). **Platform support (current version v2.0.8; matrix owner-confirmed in the wild):** **Windows** (per-user one-click NSIS) **WORKS** — first manual download trips SmartScreen ("More info → Run anyway"), then **auto-updates** end-to-end. **Linux** (AppImage) — `chmod +x` then run; on **Mint/Ubuntu** the AppImage needs the **FUSE 2** runtime (`sudo apt install libfuse2`, or `libfuse2t64` on Mint 22 / Ubuntu 24.04) or it silently no-ops — `--appimage-extract-and-run` is the FUSE-free fallback (verified working on Mint 2026-06-29). **macOS now builds BOTH arm64 AND x64** (since v2.0.7; corrects the old "arm64-only" claim — two dmgs per release: `ComfyQ-Discovery-<ver>-arm64.dmg` for Apple Silicon, `ComfyQ-Discovery-<ver>.dmg` for Intel). The mac `.app` is **AD-HOC code-signed in CI** via [desktop/scripts/afterPack.cjs](desktop/scripts/afterPack.cjs) (`codesign --force --deep --sign -`, with `mac.identity: null` + `hardenedRuntime: false` in [desktop/electron-builder.yml](desktop/electron-builder.yml) so there's exactly one signing step we control; afterPack throws on failure, so a green mac CI job proves the signature applied). **Why ad-hoc:** an *unsigned* arm64 app is a dead end — Gatekeeper rejects it outright as "damaged" with no GUI bypass; an ad-hoc signature makes it a valid runnable binary, so Gatekeeper downgrades to the gentler "unidentified developer" prompt that *does* have a no-Terminal bypass: right-click → Open → Open (Big Sur..Sonoma), or System Settings → Privacy & Security → Open Anyway (Sequoia); `xattr -cr '/Applications/ComfyQ Discovery.app'` is the universal fallback. It is **NOT notarized / NOT Developer-ID**. **macOS arm64 (Apple Silicon): WORKS** as of v2.0.8 (owner-confirmed, one-time bypass above). **macOS Intel (x64): requires macOS ≥ 12 (Monterey)** — on **macOS 11 Big Sur it is still flagged "damaged"/"endommagé" and won't open even after the bypass** (known limitation; the owner is upgrading those Intel users to macOS 12 to resolve it). **★ Ad-hoc fixes LAUNCH, not auto-update.** Squirrel.Mac (electron-updater's macOS engine) validates an update against a *stable* code-signing identity, which an ad-hoc signature has no Team ID for — so **macOS still does NOT auto-update**; on macOS, updating = install + **manual re-download** of a newer `.dmg`. **Windows + Linux remain the fully-working auto-update targets** (and remain unsigned). macOS won't silently self-update until a real **Developer-ID/notarized** signature lands (ad-hoc ≠ Developer-ID). Local `npm run dist` on Windows fails at the winCodeSign `.7z` extraction (needs the symlink-create privilege → enable **Developer Mode** or run elevated) — **CI runners are unaffected**.

**Federation — LAN fleet monitor (UDP beacon + standalone Electron app) [implemented 2026-06-27; now packaged + auto-updating (see the entry above) and entering user testing 2026-06-28; single-machine verified, two-machine test pending]** — first federation work. Built on branch `feature/federation-fleet-monitor`. Verified locally end-to-end: machine identity/GPU/RAM/IP detected, beacon→listener round-trip, `GET /federation/self` live on the running dev server, Electron app boots + renders. App is branded with the ComfyQ mark ([desktop/assets/icon.svg](desktop/assets/icon.svg) → 512px `icon.png` for the window/taskbar + electron-builder; inline SVG header logo in [desktop/renderer/index.html](desktop/renderer/index.html)). Realizes Phase-F F1+F2 via a **UDP multicast status beacon** + a **cross-platform Electron "fleet monitor"** in a new top-level `desktop/` workspace — deliberately **diverging from the originally-locked mDNS/in-browser-panel design** (owner decision; see [implementation_plan.md](implementation_plan.md) → Phase F "Implementation path chosen"). Each ComfyQ server **sends a JSON status snapshot every ~5 s** over UDP — to the multicast group `239.255.42.99:41999` **and** to each interface's directed/limited broadcast (built-in `dgram`, no transport dep). The dual send is deliberate: consumer Wi-Fi/multi-AP LANs frequently drop multicast and multi-homed machines pick the wrong default interface, so **directed broadcast is what actually makes same-subnet peers see each other** (multicast TTL is 4 to survive a router hop; receiver joins multicast on *all* interfaces, broadcast is received automatically when bound to `0.0.0.0`). Runs in **both admin and student mode** so even **idle admin rigs with nothing launched** appear; the desktop app **listens** (peer map + stale-expiry live in the app, no per-peer polling). **Note:** the lab LAN is a **/23** (`10.10.16.0`–`10.10.17.255`), so the directed broadcast is `10.10.17.255` — broadcast addr is computed from each iface's real netmask, not assumed /24. **Discovery on broadcast-blocked LANs (subnet auto-scan + static peers):** managed/school Wi-Fi (the lab's `lecolededesign.com` net, a /23 `10.10.16.0`–`10.10.17.255`) enables client isolation for broadcast+multicast, so UDP beacons never arrive even though unicast works fine (verified: ping + `curl http://<peer>:3000/federation/self` both succeed, UDP beacon doesn't). So the app discovers machines over **unicast** three ways, all merged into one peer map ([desktop/main.js](desktop/main.js)): (1) UDP beacons (`source:'beacon'`); (2) **subnet auto-scan** — sweeps the local subnet probing `http://<ip>:3000/federation/self` (concurrency 48, 1.5s timeout, every 60s; a /23 sweep ≈ 16s; `source:'scan'`); (3) **Add machine by IP** for other subnets (`source:'poll'`, persisted). Discovered + static hosts are fast-polled every 5s to stay fresh; auto-scan is on by default with a toggle + "Rescan now" and skips subnets > /20 (4096 hosts) to stay polite. All of it reuses the existing Express `:3000` (no new firewall rule — same path the web UI uses) and persists to `userData/fleet-config.json` (`{staticPeers, autoScan}`; seedable via `COMFYQ_FED_PEERS`). IPC: `get/add/remove-static-peer`, `rescan`, `set-auto-scan` ([desktop/preload.js](desktop/preload.js)). **On by default** (`config.federation.enabled:true`; a **Network presence** toggle in AdminConfig flips it **live** — beacon re-reads enabled state each tick + `runtime.beacon.kick()` sends one immediately on enable — via `PUT /admin/federation`) and **read-only** — the only action is a "Schedule a job" button that `shell.openExternal`s `http://<rig-ip>:5173`. Snapshot is built once by `buildSnapshot()` ([server/federation/statusSnapshot.js](server/federation/statusSnapshot.js)) and shared by the beacon ([server/federation/beacon.js](server/federation/beacon.js)) and a `GET /federation/self` REST fallback ([server/routes/federation.js](server/routes/federation.js)) so they can't drift. It reuses existing status sources — `lanAddresses()` ([server/index.js](server/index.js)), `comfyStatus()`/`worker.getStatus()`, `queue.list({status})` ([server/queue/jobQueue.js](server/queue/jobQueue.js)), `registry.get(id).summary` (name + `estimatedDurationSec`). Identity (UUID + name + GPU + VRAM + RAM) detected at boot via `systeminformation` (`/system_stats` can't report GPU on an idle rig) with an nvidia-smi fallback, persisted to a new `config.instance` block ([schemas.js](server/config/schemas.js)). New deps: `systeminformation` (server) and `electron` (desktop, dev — pinned `^42` to clear the <=39 advisories; **`electron-builder` is deliberately NOT a default dep** — its old transitive tree carried 10 high-sev advisories + many deprecations, so the runtime install is `electron`-only → **0 vulnerabilities**, ~13 pkgs; add it ad-hoc to build installers). `desktop/` uses a **vanilla HTML/JS renderer (no bundler)** — `npm run desktop`; installers (NSIS/dmg) deferred. **Gotcha:** Electron's binary-download postinstall can be silently skipped during `npm install` (symptom: "Electron failed to install correctly"), so `desktop:install` runs `node desktop/node_modules/electron/install.js` explicitly after the npm install. **Card content + UX (2026-06-28):** the snapshot adds a **`usage`** block — `usersConnected` (live socket count via `runtime.bus.connectedUsers` in student mode; distinct recent HTTP client IPs via a `runtime.activity` tracker in admin mode — `/federation/self` + `/socket.io` excluded so monitoring/idle connections don't count) + **`idleSec`** (time since last real activity; `runtime.activity.lastTs` bumped by non-excluded HTTP requests and by the bus on connect/book/queue-change). `activeWorkflow` now also carries **`description`** pulled **live from the workflow's `meta.json`** via `registry.get(id).summary.description` (referenced, not copied — drop a workflow in `workflows/` and its blurb shows up). The app shows plain-language state (Running a workflow / Ready / On standby), usage on **every** card, and the workflow name + description + queue + Schedule button **only when serving**; **admin-mode cards hide the workflow + Schedule** (nothing served). The subnet **scan range is user-editable** in-app (base + 3rd-octet from–to + 4th-octet from–to, defaulting to the machine's own subnet; persisted as `scanRange` in `fleet-config.json`, capped 4096/sweep) via `get/set-scan-range` IPC. Peer `source` values are `beacon` / `scan` (auto-found) / `added` (manual). **More UX (2026-06-28 b):** (a) **"This machine"** marker — `main.js` includes `selfIps` (its own IPv4s) in the payload; the card whose `ips` intersect gets a badge + accent border. (b) **Copy admin link** — a copy button by the IP copies `http://<ip>:5173/admin` via a `copy-text` IPC (`electron.clipboard`) with a toast (renderer clipboard is unreliable under file://). (c) **ComfyUI backend on by default** — admin boot calls `adminCalibrator.launchBackend()` (network-bound, non-blocking) when `comfy_ui.autoStart!==false` + paths set, so an idle rig is "engine on" and shows `comfy.running` ([server/index.js](server/index.js) admin block). (d) discovery controls (auto-search toggle / range / add-IP) moved **behind a ⚙ Settings button** (the `.controls` panel toggles `hidden`). (e) the **"Now serving" + workflow name banner moved up** directly under the IP, led by an icon matching the **admin library's filter-chip (group) icons** — lucide SVGs inlined in `renderer.js`; a fine-grained category is mapped to a group via the same `CATEGORY_GROUP` as WorkflowSelector, then to its **group** icon (`GROUP_ICON`): 3D→box, Audio→music, Description→file-text, Image generation→**wand**, Video generation→video, Utilities→**wrench**, else→grid (the icon's tooltip is the group label). Mirrors the chips, not the per-card category icons. **Fallback:** if a peer runs an older server that doesn't send `category` (pre-`usage`/`description` builds), `groupFromId()` infers the group from the workflow id (utility checks — upscale/seedvr2/segment/interpolation — run before image/video so the seedvr2 upscalers land in Utilities), so the icon is still correct; `usage`/`description` can't be inferred and need the peer's server updated. **Styling + naming (2026-06-28 c):** the app is renamed **"ComfyQ – Discovery"** (window title, header, `productName`/`appId` `com.comfyq.discovery`) and restyled to **match the web client** — `desktop/renderer/styles.css` now uses the same zinc tokens as [client/src/index.css](client/src/index.css) (dark + `.light` on `:root`), the Inter font stack, and a **light/dark toggle** (sun/moon in the header; honors `prefers-color-scheme`, persists to `localStorage`); accent is the ComfyQ grey `primary`, not blue. **Bug fixed:** the settings panel showed by default because `.controls{display:flex}` (defined after `.hidden`) overrode it — `.hidden` is now `display:none !important`, so the ⚙ Settings panel is truly hidden until opened. **Render fix (2026-06-28 d):** the grid no longer does a full `appEl.innerHTML` rebuild every 1s — that was destroying the tiny copy button between mousedown/mouseup so its clicks were eaten (the big Schedule button survived, so it looked like "copy doesn't work"). Now cards rebuild **only when a content signature changes** (`cardsSignature()` excludes time-volatile fields + uses a 30s idle bucket; heartbeats don't rebuild); relative-time text is updated in place by `tickTimes()` each second and per-push `data-ts`/stale are patched onto existing cards; all clicks use **event delegation** on stable parents (`appEl`/`peerChipsEl`). The clipboard write itself goes through the `copy-text` IPC → `electron.clipboard` (always worked). **Cross-network discovery + scan reliability (2026-06-29, v2.0.9):** the unicast IP-range scan ([desktop/main.js](desktop/main.js)) was rebuilt to fix "machines sometimes don't show up" and to find servers on a *different* subnet than the client. Three changes: (a) **ignore virtual adapters** (`VIRTUAL_IFACE_RX` skips Hyper-V/WSL `vEthernet (Default Switch)`, VM/VPN/container NICs) — `localIPv4s()[0]` was non-deterministically picking the Hyper-V switch (`172.19.240.1/20`), so the sweep targeted a dead virtual range and the *bad range got persisted* to `fleet-config.json`, sticking until reset (the intermittency); (b) scan **every real local subnet** via `localRanges()`, not just the first interface; (c) scan **seeded extra ranges** on top — `DEFAULT_EXTRA_SCAN` (the lab's `edna-sdc` server subnet `10.10.16.0/23`), the `COMFYQ_FED_SCAN` env (comma-sep CIDRs/ranges), and a `scanRanges` array in `fleet-config.json` — so a client on the `asus` 192.168.50.0/24 router-behind-the-LAN auto-finds the GPU rigs it can't reach by broadcast. Candidates are de-duplicated into a Set + capped at 4096; the auto-derived local range is no longer persisted (only explicit user/seeded ranges are). The in-app **Search range** field is now an *additional* range, not a replacement — local subnets are always scanned. `parseRangeSpec()` accepts `10.10.16.0/23`, `10.10.16-17.1-254`, or `10.10.16.x`. **★ Hard limit:** this only works if the client's network actually *routes* to the server subnet (e.g. the asus router's WAN is uplinked to edna-sdc → outbound NAT reaches `10.10.17.x`; verify with `curl http://<server>:3000/federation/self`). Across two isolated networks no scan can bridge them — true zero-config discovery only happens when all machines share one broadcast-friendly LAN (the asus LAN has no client isolation; edna-sdc/`lecolededesign.com` blocks broadcast+multicast, which is why the unicast scan exists). **Still deferred:** mDNS, cluster-secret auth, cross-instance admin actions, the AdminConfig panel, the student peer-picker, the orchestrator role.

**Admin "Open in ComfyUI" stages the editable workflow into ComfyUI's Workflows menu (2026-07-01)** — a bundle can now carry an optional **`<id>_template.json`** — the *original ComfyUI UI/litegraph export* (subgraphs, groups, notes, layout) the api.json was derived from. **Open in ComfyUI** now boots ComfyUI and **`POST /admin/workflows/:id/stage-template` copies that template into `<comfy root>/user/default/workflows/<id>.json`**, so it shows up in ComfyUI's **Workflows sidebar** — one click to open as a **fully editable graph** (`loadGraphData`, subgraphs intact) instead of the flat API graph. This closes the 2026-06-26 "Open in ComfyUI" gap and automates what the owner did by hand (verified: that dir already held a hand-copied `3d_triposplat_..._.json`). **Why staging, not a URL:** the installed **ComfyUI 1.45.20** frontend reads only `oauth/tier/cycle/template/source/mode` query params — there's **no `?workflow=` to auto-open an arbitrary user workflow** (`?template=` loads *registered* templates only), so the sidebar is the reliable, version-independent handoff. Wiring: registry detects the file (`entry.hasTemplate`/`templatePath` in [workflowRegistry.js](server/workflows/workflowRegistry.js) — **not parsed**, since the parser only reads API format), and detection is **tolerant of filename variance** (`_loadOne` falls back to any `*template.json` in the folder — a defensive net; a few were initially named `.template.json`/`video_*`/shortened but **all 23 are now renamed to the canonical `<id>_template.json`**, 2026-07-01); `GET /workflows/:id/edit-data` returns `templateWorkflow` + `hasTemplate`, and `hasTemplate` is also on the `/workflows` **summary**. The opener logic lives in a shared hook [useComfyOpener.js](client/src/hooks/useComfyOpener.js) (`openInComfy(id)` → status/launch → `stage-template` → open tab, **falling back to download+drag** when staging 409s/fails), used by **both** the editor modal and — the main change this round — a **per-card "Open in ComfyUI" button** in the admin library ([WorkflowSelector.jsx](client/src/components/WorkflowSelector.jsx)), so it's reachable from the admin panel without opening the editor. The same per-card row adds an **"Activate & serve"** button (`activate(id)` → `/admin/activate-workflow` → student mode; the active card shows a green **"Serving"** state) so an admin can launch the API version — what **ComfyQ Discovery** shows being served — straight from the card (also still in the bottom action bar, acting on the selected workflow). **All 23 non-candidate bundles now ship a template**; the registry **skips `_`-prefixed helper folders** (e.g. the new `workflows/_candidate_workflows/` staging area) so candidates don't show as broken bundles. Staging overwrites `<id>.json` each click (bundle template is the source of truth); zero change to the run/materialize path (the api.json still executes).

**LTX 2.3 text → video, auto-prompt (2026-06-26; ⚠ removed 2026-09-14 — superseded by [video_ltx2_5_t2v](workflows/video_ltx2_5_t2v/))** — `video_ltx2_3_t2v`, the **pure text-to-video** member of the LTX 2.3 family (no input — a built-in `EmptyImage 267:325` stands in for the i2v image path; the graph's `267:201` "Switch to Text to Video?" boolean is pinned true). Same template/node-IDs as [video_ltx2_3_i2v](workflows/video_ltx2_3_i2v/) (shared `267:*` ids) plus the **Gemma prompt-enhancer** chain ([video_ltx2_3_ia2v_iclora](workflows/video_ltx2_3_ia2v_iclora/)-style): `PrimitiveStringMultiline 267:266` → `TextGenerateLTX2Prompt 267:327` → `ComfySwitchNode 267:329` (gated by the `267:330` **Enhance prompt with AI** toggle) → the real `267:240 CLIPTextEncode`. LTX 2.3 22B two-stage `SamplerCustomAdvanced` + spatial upscaler + audio VAE (same engine as the IC-LoRA sibling). Exposes prompt / **Enhance prompt with AI** / negative / **duration (seconds)** / **width** / **height** / **fps** / seed — width+height exposed (unlike the image-input siblings) since t2v has no input image to set the aspect ratio. Pruned the prompt-preview `PreviewAny 267:328` (it mirrors the switch output `267:329` that already feeds `267:240`, so it's a pure OUTPUT_NODE that would dump the final/enhanced prompt into the job gallery). 50 nodes, all reachable from `SaveVideo`; 5 models (same set as the IC-LoRA sibling); `i2v`→Video group; cache-immune calibration (two real `noise_seed`s + `sampling_mode.seed` all randomized). Zero server code. Pending rig verification.

**Workflow editor — live student-form preview (2026-06-26)** — editing parameters in [WorkflowMetaEditor.jsx](client/src/components/admin/WorkflowMetaEditor.jsx) now has a **"Student preview"** toggle that renders, live and side-by-side, the exact booking form students will see — updating as the admin changes labels / types / defaults / options / order / enable-disable (the modal widens to `max-w-7xl` while shown). To guarantee the preview **can't drift** from the real thing, the student field-rendering was extracted out of [BookingDialog.jsx](client/src/components/BookingDialog.jsx) into a shared [DynamicParamFields.jsx](client/src/components/DynamicParamFields.jsx): BookingDialog (real booking) and the editor preview both render that one component from the same `parameter_map` shape. The preview builds the map from the editor's **unsaved** params (enabled only, in the admin's order) and drives it with throwaway local state — re-seeded only on structural/default/options/type changes (not on label/order edits, so renaming/reordering doesn't reset it), seeds preserved across re-seeds (no flicker), media previews via `FileReader`. Helpers `isSeedParam`/`randomSeed`/`prettyInputName` moved to the shared module (still imported by BookingDialog's form-init). **Bonus fix:** `disabledWhen` field-gating now **survives an editor open+save** — it's added to the `edit-data` merge ([admin.js](server/routes/admin.js)) and the editor's save payload (previously the editor silently dropped it, so a re-save of an either/or-prompt workflow like `video_edit_ltx2_3_ic_lora_vid2vid` lost the gating); the zod `ExposedParameter` already allowed it. Verified by an adversarial multi-agent pass (render fidelity / preview wiring / round-trip) — zero behavioral divergence.

**Removed per-job time budgets + the ID-LoRA talking-head bundle (2026-06-26)** — (1) **No runtime budget on jobs.** The `maxRuntimeSec` deadline that auto-failed a running job with `runtime-budget-exceeded` once it ran past its meta's max runtime is **gone** — a job now runs until ComfyUI finishes **or a user/admin cancels** it (the X-button → `cancel_job` → `rest.interrupt()` path is the only kill, by design — the owner wants a human to decide when a long job is "too long"). Removed: the executor's `_historyDeadline` ([jobExecutor.js](server/executor/jobExecutor.js)), the `maxRuntimeSec` field from `WorkflowMeta` ([schemas.js](server/config/schemas.js)), the editor's "Max runtime" field ([WorkflowMetaEditor.jsx](client/src/components/admin/WorkflowMetaEditor.jsx)), the upload-scaffold default ([admin.js](server/routes/admin.js)), the `Worker.submit` opt ([workerInterface.js](server/workers/workerInterface.js)), and the field stripped from **all 25 bundle metas**. **Calibration keeps a fixed safety cap** (`CALIBRATION_TIMEOUT_MS` = 2h in [benchmarkService.js](server/benchmark/benchmarkService.js)) so a stuck admin-gauge run can't hang the worker forever — that's a *gauge* bound, not a per-job budget. *(Earlier progress entries below that cite `maxRuntimeSec 1800/1200/600/…` describe now-removed metadata — the numbers no longer exist.)* (2) **Deleted** `workflows/video_ltx2_3_id_lora_img_audio2vid/` (the LTX 2.3 ID-LoRA lip-sync talking-head) at the owner's request; its non-identity IC-LoRA sibling [video_ltx2_3_ia2v_iclora](workflows/video_ltx2_3_ia2v_iclora/) remains.

**ComfyUI failure messages — friendly "no face detected" + a humanizer hook (2026-06-26)** — LivePortrait (and any face-cropper workflow) failed with a raw, opaque ComfyUI exception (e.g. `list index out of range` off `LivePortraitCropper`) when the input image has no detectable face. New [errorMessages.js](server/executor/errorMessages.js) `humanizeFailure(message, nodeType)` maps known failure signatures to clear, actionable text — a **face-detection failure** (failing node matches `cropper`/`insightface`/`mediapipe`/`facedetect`, **or** the message mentions no/zero/undetected face) → *"No face was detected in the image. Please upload a clear photo with a single, fully visible face…"*. Applied at **both** error sites — the WS `execution_error` event ([localComfyUIWorker.js](server/workers/localComfyUIWorker.js)) and the history-status fallback ([jobExecutor.js](server/executor/jobExecutor.js)) — both of which carry `node_type`. Guards against false positives: an **OOM** on a face node is passed through verbatim (`MEMORY_RX`), and any unmatched error returns its raw message (never hidden). Extensible — add a `(signature → message)` pair for the next confusing error class.

**Admin workflow editor — parameter search + Open-in-ComfyUI + auto-start (2026-06-26)** — [WorkflowMetaEditor.jsx](client/src/components/admin/WorkflowMetaEditor.jsx) gained two things. (1) A **parameter search** (multi-term AND over display name / key / field / node id / node title / class type / type) — essential on big graphs (the Bernini autoprompt surfaces **106** detected widgets); each row now also shows the node's **`_meta.title`** (e.g. `node 117:54 · Custom Combo · choice`). The parser emits `nodeTitle`/`classType` per detected param ([workflowParser.js](server/workflows/workflowParser.js)) — display/search only, **not** persisted into `exposedParameters` (the zod schema strips them on save). (2) An **Open in ComfyUI** button that **auto-starts ComfyUI if it isn't running** (POSTs `/admin/comfyui/launch`, which blocks until the backend is reachable) then opens its editor tab, plus a **Download JSON** button; `edit-data` now also returns `apiWorkflow` for the client-side blob download. The tab + download fire **synchronously inside the click** (so the browser doesn't block the popup/download), and the tab is steered to ComfyUI after the async launch. **★ ComfyUI constraint:** ComfyUI only auto-imports **API-format** JSON via **drag-drop** (its file handler's `loadApiJson`); the `?workflow=`/sidebar paths feed litegraph `configure()`, which expects ComfyUI's own **UI/litegraph** format and silently yields an empty graph for API JSON — so there is **no cross-version URL that loads our API-format bundles**. Hence the one-drag flow (open + hand over the file). True zero-drag would require a server-side API→UI converter (fetch `/object_info` for slot/widget ordering) — deferred as version-fragile.

**LTX 2.3 IC-LoRA image + audio → video, auto-prompt (2026-06-26)** — [video_ltx2_3_ia2v_iclora](workflows/video_ltx2_3_ia2v_iclora/), the **IC-LoRA / non-identity counterpart** to what was the LTX 2.3 ID-LoRA talking-head (`video_ltx2_3_id_lora_img_audio2vid`, **since removed** — see the entry above). Character image (`269`) + reference audio (`276`) → talking video (LTX 2.3 22B, two-stage `SamplerCustomAdvanced` + spatial upscaler, audio VAE encode/decode). Uses the **distilled** LoRA + a Gemma **prompt-enhancer** (`TextGenerateLTX2Prompt` `340:346`, gated by a `340:349` toggle that rewrites the user prompt into an LTX-optimized one) instead of an identity LoRA — so it's the creative/non-identity variant. Exposes image / audio / prompt / **Enhance prompt with AI** / negative / duration / seed. Pruned the prompt-preview `PreviewAny` sink (`340:347` — it mirrors the switch output `340:348` that already feeds the real `340:306` CLIPTextEncode, so it's a pure OUTPUT_NODE that would dump the final prompt into the job gallery). 54 nodes, all reachable from `SaveVideo`; 5 models; `i2v`→Video group; cache-immune (real `noise_seed` + `sampling_mode.seed`). **Naming note:** the unrelated `video_edit_ltx2_3_ic_lora_vid2vid` (⚠ removed 2026-09-14) is a *third* workflow — an IC-LoRA **depth video→video** restyle (`ic-lora-union-control` + MoGe) — confirmed with the owner it stays as-is (no rename). Pending rig verification.

**Mask-draw inpainting — new `mask` param type + in-browser mask painter (2026-06-25)** — the first workflow input where the user *paints* a mask instead of uploading a file. Two Flux.2 Klein inpainting bundles: `image_flux2_inpaint` (paint a mask + prompt) and `image_flux2_inpaint_with_reference` (adds a reference image, the look/subject/object to bring in). **⚠ These two originals were later removed in the workflow-tree cleanup and re-added 2026-07-05 as [image_edit_flux2_klein_inpaint_prompt](workflows/image_edit_flux2_klein_inpaint_prompt/) / [image_edit_flux2_klein_inpaint_reference](workflows/image_edit_flux2_klein_inpaint_reference/)** (see the 2026-07-05 Latest-progress entries — the re-added graphs use node ids `1`/`22`, not the `76`/`105` this dated note describes; the `mask` infra below is unchanged). The graph's `76 LoadImage` provides **both** the base (IMAGE out 0) and the mask (MASK out 1 = `1−alpha`), so **one painted RGBA PNG carries both** — no graph rewiring; node `105` is a plain reference-image upload. New **`mask` param type** — its wire value is just an image filename, so it rides the generic infra (materializer / `/upload` / recall / calibration), only the *widget* differs. Touchpoints: `ParamType` enum ([schemas.js](server/config/schemas.js)), parser `inferType` + required-by-default ([workflowParser.js](server/workflows/workflowParser.js)), admin meta editor type list + `maxInputEdge` case ([WorkflowMetaEditor.jsx](client/src/components/admin/WorkflowMetaEditor.jsx)), BookingDialog media set + render branch, and calibration stages a plain image for `mask` (empty mask → no-op inpaint but the 4-step sampler still runs → valid timing) ([benchmarkService.js](server/benchmark/benchmarkService.js)). New component [MaskDrawField.jsx](client/src/components/capture/MaskDrawField.jsx): pick a base image → brush over the region to replace (brush size / eraser / undo / clear / colour swatches + custom picker) → composites `drawImage(base)` then `globalCompositeOperation='destination-out' drawImage(mask)` so painted pixels become **transparent** → emits an `image/png` File up the **existing** upload path (`onChange`→`handleMediaChange`). **Alpha survives end-to-end** (server never re-encodes — multer raw bytes, `image-size` is read-only; client `resizeImageFile` keeps PNG→PNG, passes small images through). **Brush colour is purely cosmetic** — export reads only alpha; recolour is `source-in` (replaces RGB, preserves the alpha geometry) so the output PNG is byte-identical regardless of colour. Pruned the rgthree `Image Comparer` (+ `MaskPreview+` in the ref variant) — OUTPUT_NODE sinks. Category `image-edit`; models flux-2-klein-9b-fp8 / qwen_3_8b_fp8mixed / flux2-vae; fast (4-step distilled). **★ Rig-watch: mask polarity** — confirm only the *painted* region changes (standard ComfyUI white=inpaint; the graph's `mask_invert:false` confirms it) — if inverted, flip in `MaskDrawField.commit()`. Pending rig verification.

**Admin workflow library — keyword search (2026-06-25)** — [WorkflowSelector.jsx](client/src/components/WorkflowSelector.jsx) gained a search box above the type-filter chips. Multi-term AND match over name / description / tags / category / id; runs **before** the group filter so the chip counts (and the "All" count) reflect the current query and empty groups drop out. A "no matches" empty state offers **Clear filters** (resets search + group). `tags` is confirmed present in the `/workflows` summary, so tag search works.

**Wan 2.2 Bernini-R video editing with reference — auto-prompt (2026-06-25)** — [video_edit_bernini_r_video_editing_ref_autoprompt](workflows/video_edit_bernini_r_video_editing_ref_autoprompt/), the auto-prompting sibling of `..._with_reference`: instead of typing the instruction, a **Gemma-4 chain writes it** — one `TextGenerate` describes the source video (`131`), another the reference image (`129`); two `StringConcatenate`s fold them with a system instruction (`134`/`135`); a third `TextGenerate` (`133`) composes the final Bernini edit prompt → `PrimitiveStringMultiline 117:114` → the positive conditioning (`117:59`→`117:3`). So the user supplies only **video + reference image** (+ task / fast-mode / clip-length / seed; **no instruction field** — for a hand-written instruction use the plain `..._with_reference` bundle). Same Bernini dual high/low-noise sampler + Turbo "Fast mode" + subset task `select` (Video Editing with Reference / Ads-Content Insertion) + the kept in-path line-index `PreviewAny` (`117:57:1`). Pruning: the frame-count `PreviewAny` sink (`127`) plus the **two description `PreviewAny`s** (`130`/`132`) — those are *in-path passthroughs* feeding `134`, so `134` was rewired straight to `129`/`131` and they were dropped (else two long auto-descriptions would land in the job gallery). **6 models** (adds the `gemma4_e4b_it_fp8_scaled` CLIP for the autoprompt). 48 nodes, all reachable from `SaveVideo`. Category `i2v`; `maxRuntimeSec 1800`. Calibration cache-immune (literal `noise_seed` + every `sampling_mode.seed` randomized). **Rig-watch**: confirm the autoprompt still assembles after the `130`/`132` bypass, and whether `117:57:1` emits a stray line-index caption. Pending rig verification.

**Admin path ergonomics — drive-letter swap + ComfyUI auto-detect (2026-06-24)** — workshop machines clone the same drive image to different letters, so the ComfyUI paths constantly need re-pointing. Two fixes in [AdminConfig.jsx](client/src/pages/AdminConfig.jsx) + [configManager.js](server/config/configManager.js): (1) a **Drive letter** dropdown in ComfyUI Settings rewrites the drive on *every* absolute path at once (root / python / output / assets; relative paths untouched) — populated from mounted drives via `GET /admin/drives`. (2) **Auto-detect**: `configManager.detectComfyPaths()` scans local drives for the portable layout (a dir with `main.py` + sibling `python_embeded`); it runs **on boot** ([index.js](server/index.js)) whenever the configured root has no `main.py` (stale / cloned-drive / fresh-clone) and adopts the found install — so `npm run dev` self-heals — and is also a manual **Auto-detect** button (`POST /admin/autodetect-paths`). Bounded scan (drive enum + one readdir/drive + a few `existsSync`), Windows-only, and it **never overrides a valid config**.

**Student-UX fixes — light theme, clipboard, asset reuse, ASAP booking (2026-06-24)** — a batch of small fixes: **(a) Light-theme contrast** — the `--color-white → --text` compat remap (see Serving model) renders `text-white`/`bg-white` on a *solid accent fill* dark-on-dark (primary buttons, active tabs, toggle knob, the text-caption lightbox panel). Added a `--color-on-primary` token (stays light in both modes) for those spots, and the caption panel now uses `bg-background`/`text-foreground` instead of a hardcoded black box. **(b) Copy text** for caption jobs silently failed on plain-HTTP LAN (`navigator.clipboard` is undefined off a secure context) → new [clipboard.js](client/src/utils/clipboard.js) helper with a hidden-`<textarea>` + `execCommand('copy')` fallback. **(c) "Use these settings" reuses media** — uploaded inputs land in `job.params` and *aren't* TTL-swept (`sweepStale` only matches `comfyq__`, not the `comfyq_session__` upload prefix), so the booking dialog re-binds the original filename (no re-upload) and previews/plays it via a new `GET /input-media/:filename` route ([uploads.js](server/routes/uploads.js)). **(d) ASAP booking** — booking with no slot picked (or a past time) now queues into the **next free slot** (`jobQueue.nextFreeSlot()`, no collision rejection) instead of erroring; the dialog has a visible **As soon as possible / Pick a time** segmented toggle.

**Wan 2.2 Bernini-R video editing (text-only + with reference image) — verified on rig (2026-06-21)** — two bundles from one unified Bernini-R graph: [video_edit_bernini_r_video_editing](workflows/video_edit_bernini_r_video_editing/) (source video + instruction → edited/restyled video) and [video_edit_bernini_r_video_editing_with_reference](workflows/video_edit_bernini_r_video_editing_with_reference/) (adds a reference image, wired into `BerniniConditioning.reference_images.reference_image_0` via `LoadImage`→`BatchImagesNode`). Wan 2.2 **dual high/low-noise** diffusion (`SamplerCustom`×2 + `SplitSigmas`) with a **Turbo-LoRA "Fast mode"** checkbox (a `PrimitiveBoolean` driving four `ComfySwitchNode`s — model/steps/CFG/split-steps: 6-step/CFG1 fast vs 40-step/CFG5 quality); source audio + fps carried through (`GetVideoComponents`→`CreateVideo`). **Task dropdown** (`CustomCombo`): its *index* output selects one line from a 12-line system-prompt block prepended to the user instruction — exposed as a **subset `select`** limited to the actually-wired tasks (no-ref: Video Editing / Content Propagation / Action-Position / Style-Motion; ref: with Reference / Ads-Content Insertion). **Zero server code.** Pruning: removed the dead `LoadImage`+`BatchImages` island (no-ref only — it's the *live* reference path in the ref variant) + the diagnostic frame-count `PreviewAny`; **kept** the in-path line-index `PreviewAny` (`76:57:1`, it coerces the combo index to a string for `StringReplace`) which may emit a stray index caption (rig-watch item). Heavy (dual 14B fp8) → `maxRuntimeSec 1800`; real `noise_seed` → cache-immune calibration. Category `i2v` → **Video generation** group. The reference variant is the first **image-INPUT + video-INPUT → video** on a Wan diffusion-edit graph (generic materializer injects both `47.file` video and `104.image` reference with no new code).

**Wan 2.2 Bernini-R image editing (text-only + with reference image) — verified on rig (2026-06-21)** — the single-image counterparts of the video pair above, from the same Bernini-R graph: [image_edit_bernini_r_image_editing](workflows/image_edit_bernini_r_image_editing/) (image + instruction → edited image) and [image_edit_bernini_r_image_editing_with_reference](workflows/image_edit_bernini_r_image_editing_with_reference/) (adds a reference image via the same `LoadImage`→`BatchImagesNode`→`BerniniConditioning.reference_images.reference_image_0` path). The source image is fed to `BerniniConditioning` as a **1-frame "video"** (`length:1`, dims from `GetImageSize` off a `ResizeImageMaskNode`→1280 long-edge) → dual high/low-noise sampling → `VAEDecode` → `SaveImage`. Same Turbo-LoRA "Fast mode" + a subset task `select` (no-ref: Image Editing / Default; ref: Image Editing / Subject to Image — this `CustomCombo` has only **5** task options vs the video graph's 12; literal aligned to "Image Editing"/idx 3 so the dropdown degrades gracefully). Pruning: the dead `LoadImage`+`BatchImages` island (no-ref) and the diagnostic **`ImageCompare`** before/after node (`118`, an OUTPUT_NODE → would have dropped the original + result into the job gallery); kept the in-path line-index `PreviewAny` (same stray-caption rig-watch). Category `image-edit` → **Image generation** group; zero server code.

**Admin workflow library — filter by type (2026-06-21)** — the admin workflow picker ([WorkflowSelector.jsx](client/src/components/WorkflowSelector.jsx)) now filters by a user-facing **group taxonomy** rendered as chips: **3D / Audio / Description / Image generation / Video generation / Utilities** (+ Other). A `CATEGORY_GROUP` map collapses the fine-grained meta `category` into these buckets — `t2i`/`image-edit`→image, `i2v`→video, `i2i`+`preprocessor`→utilities (upscalers, segmentation, frame-interpolation, depth), `3d`/`audio`/`description` pass through. Chips only render for groups that have a usable workflow (with counts); replaced the old raw-category dropdown. Added a new **`description` category** to the enum ([schemas.js](server/config/schemas.js)) + meta editor + server label map, and **reclassified the two Gemma captioners `preprocessor`→`description`** so "describe-it" tools group apart from utilities.

**ComfyUI "Launch on the network" used a stale path — fixed (2026-06-21)** — the admin *Launch ComfyUI backend* button spawned ComfyUI with the **boot-time** config snapshot (often the hardcoded `defaultConfig()` portable path), ignoring a root/python path edited + saved in Settings just before. [AdminCalibrator](server/benchmark/adminCalibrator.js) (owns that button via `runtime.comfyBackend`) now reads `comfy_ui`/`queue`/`assets` **fresh from `configManager`** on every use (getters, not a constructor snapshot — config.json is the source of truth), **restarts a ComfyUI it owns when the saved paths change** (tracked via a `root|python|port` spawn signature), and logs the path it boots. The client ([AdminConfig.jsx](client/src/pages/AdminConfig.jsx) `launchComfy`) also **persists the path form before launching** so unsaved edits are honored. An externally-attached ComfyUI is still left alone.

**Gemma 4 "describe-it" captioners (image→text, video→text) — video verified on rig (2026-06-21)** — two vision-language bundles whose output is **text, not media**: [describe_gemma4_image_description](workflows/describe_gemma4_image_description/) (image → caption) and [describe_gemma4_video_description](workflows/describe_gemma4_video_description/) (video → caption, **verified on rig**). Both run Gemma 4 (`gemma4_e4b_it_fp8_scaled` via `CLIPLoader` → `TextGenerate`) and surface the result through a `PreviewAny` "Preview as Text" node. **First text-output workflows** — the image variant drove the generic **text-output path** (`outputCollector._collectText` collects a node's `text`/`string` UI array as `kind:'text'`, carried inline on the wire with no file; `ImageLightbox`/`MediaPreview` render the caption — see the new pattern below). The **video** variant (zero-code) is the first `LoadVideo`→`GetVideoComponents`→`TextGenerate` chain — every decoded frame is fed to the VLM, so keep clips short; pruned the export's two **disconnected** loaders (`LoadImage`+`LoadAudio`) so the parser doesn't surface phantom image+audio widgets and the calibrator doesn't demand assets the graph never uses. Both now categorised **`description`** (the new type added this session — they were briefly `preprocessor`). Both carry a real LLM seed (`sampling_mode.seed`), so calibration is **cache-immune** (unlike SAM3/FILM). See the off-queue entry in the roadmap below.

**SeedVR2 upscalers (image→4K, video→HD) registered + verified on rig (2026-06-17)** — see the off-queue entry in the roadmap below. Diffusion super-resolution; first **`i2i`-category** workflow; zero new code, verbatim graphs.

**Frame-interpolation utility (FILM) registered + verified on rig (2026-06-17)** — see the off-queue entry in the roadmap below. Video → smoother (or slow-motion) via FILM frame interpolation (`FrameInterpolate`); a second `preprocessor`-category utility; zero new code, zero graph edits.

**SAM3 video-segmentation utility registered + verified on rig (2026-06-16)** — see the off-queue entry in the roadmap below. Text-prompted video matte tool (Segment Anything 3): name an object → black/white mask video. First **`preprocessor`-category** workflow; zero new code.

**LivePortrait portrait-animation registered + verified on rig (2026-06-15)** — see the off-queue entry in the roadmap below. First workflow combining an **image INPUT + a video INPUT**, and the first to use **`VHS_VideoCombine`** as its output node; zero new code.

**All target workflows registered (2026-06-13 milestone; ⚠ 3 later removed/moved in the cleanup — see notes):**
- **TripoSplat** (image→3D Gaussian splat + mesh) ✅ verified on rig
- **Qwen multi-angle** (1 image → 8 views) ✅ with N-image gallery — **⚠ moved to `_candidate_workflows/` staging, not currently bundled**
- **Stable Audio 3** (text → audio) ✅ with audio player + subfolder-serving fix
